          <text x="415" y="49" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11">C₃=4µF</text>
          <line x1="400" y1="45" x2="470" y2="45" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>

          <line x1="330" y1="85" x2="380" y2="85" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
          <line x1="385" y1="75" x2="385" y2="95" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="4"/>
          <line x1="395" y1="75" x2="395" y2="95" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="4"/>
          <text x="415" y="89" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11">C₄=2µF</text>
          <line x1="400" y1="85" x2="470" y2="85" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>

          <line x1="330" y1="105" x2="390" y2="105" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
          <line x1="410" y1="105" x2="470" y2="105" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
          <line x1="390" y1="95" x2="390" y2="115" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="3"/>
          <line x1="410" y1="100" x2="410" y2="110" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="5"/>
          <text x="400" y="130" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5C6B7A">V = 10 V</text>
        </svg>
      </div>

      <ul class="q-list">
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">1.</span>For the series circuit shown (\( C_1 = 6 \, \mu\text{F} \), \( C_2 = 3 \, \mu\text{F} \), supply \( V = 18 \text{ V} \)), calculate the total (equivalent) capacitance.
          <span class="given"><strong>Shortcut Hint:</strong> For two series capacitors, use \( C_{\text{total}} = \frac{C_1 \times C_2}{C_1 + C_2} \)</span>
        </span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">2.</span>Using your answer above, calculate the total charge supplied by the battery, and state the charge stored on \( C_1 \) and on \( C_2 \).
          <span class="given"><strong>Hint:</strong> Find \( Q_{\text{total}} \) first (\( Q = C_{\text{total}} \times V \)). Remember, in series, \( Q_1 = Q_2 = Q_{\text{total}} \).</span>
        </span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">3.</span>Find the voltage across \( C_1 \) and across \( C_2 \) individually, and check that they add up to 18 V.</span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">4.</span>For the parallel circuit shown (\( C_3 = 4 \, \mu\text{F} \), \( C_4 = 2 \, \mu\text{F} \), supply \( V = 10 \text{ V} \)), calculate the total capacitance and the charge stored on each capacitor.
          <span class="given"><strong>Hint:</strong> In parallel, voltages are equal (\( V_3 = V_4 = 10 \text{ V} \)). Find \( Q \) for each using \( Q = C \times V \)</span>
        </span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">5.</span>In two sentences, explain why series combinations always give a smaller total capacitance while parallel combinations always give a larger one.</span></li>
      </ul>
      <a class="ref-note" href="index.html#series-parallel">↳ Refer to "Capacitors in Series and Parallel" on the Learning Page</a>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="stage energy" id="stage-energy">
    <div class="stage-head">
      <div class="left"><div class="stage-plate">U</div><h2>Stage 3 — Energy Storage Calculations</h2></div>
      <span class="stage-time">15 minutes</span>
    </div>
    <div class="stage-body">
      <p class="intro">Use \( U = \frac{1}{2}QV = \frac{1}{2}CV^2 = \frac{Q^2}{2C} \) to find stored energy.</p>
      <ul class="q-list">
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">1.</span>A 220 µF capacitor is charged to 25 V. Calculate the energy stored.
          <span class="given"><strong>Given:</strong> \( C = 220 \times 10^{-6} \text{ F} \), \( V = 25 \text{ V} \rightarrow \) Use \( U = \frac{1}{2}CV^2 \)</span>
        </span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">2.</span>A capacitor stores \( 4 \times 10^{-3} \text{ C} \) of charge at a potential difference of 40 V. Calculate the energy stored, using a different formula from question 1.
          <span class="given"><strong>Given:</strong> \( Q = 4 \times 10^{-3} \text{ C} \), \( V = 40 \text{ V} \rightarrow \) Use \( U = \frac{1}{2}QV \)</span>
        </span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">3.</span>Using your results from Stage 2 question 1 (the series combination), calculate the total energy stored in that circuit.</span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">4.</span>Using your results from Stage 2 question 4 (the parallel combination), calculate the total energy stored in that circuit.</span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">5.</span>Between the series and parallel circuits in Stage 2, which one stores more total energy? Suggest a reason why, based on their equivalent capacitance.</span></li>
      </ul>
      <a class="ref-note" href="index.html#energy">↳ Refer to "Energy Stored in a Capacitor" on the Learning Page</a>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="stage apply" id="stage-apply">
    <div class="stage-head">
      <div class="left"><div class="stage-plate">⚡</div><h2>Stage 4 — Real-World Application</h2></div>
      <span class="stage-time">10 minutes</span>
    </div>
    <div class="stage-body">
      <p class="intro">Reasoning questions — write full-sentence explanations, not just one-word answers.</p>
      <ul class="q-list">
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">1.</span>A camera flash uses a capacitor rather than powering the flash bulb directly from the battery. Explain why, using the idea of charge and discharge rate.</span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">2.</span>A defibrillator needs to deliver a large amount of energy to a patient in a fraction of a second. Explain why a capacitor is better suited to this job than a battery alone.</span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">3.</span>Engineers designing a phone touchscreen's capacitor-based sensors need to choose a dielectric material carefully. Suggest one property that dielectric should have, and explain how it affects the capacitance.</span></li>
      </ul>
      <a class="ref-note" href="index.html#applications">↳ Refer to "Where You'll Find Capacitors" on the Learning Page</a>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="stage reflect" id="stage-reflect">
    <div class="stage-head">
      <div class="left"><div class="stage-plate">✓</div><h2>Reflection &amp; Self-Check</h2></div>
      <span class="stage-time">5 minutes</span>
    </div>
    <div class="stage-body">
      <p class="intro">Close the loop on today's learning.</p>
      <ul class="q-list">
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">1.</span>Write one idea from today that finally "clicked" for you, and one question you still have about capacitors.</span></li>
        <li class="q-item"><input type="checkbox" class="q-check"><span class="q-text"><span class="num">2.</span>Rate your confidence (1–5) on each: (a) the definition \( C = \frac{Q}{V} \), (b) the parallel-plate formula, (c) series &amp; parallel combinations, (d) energy stored. Write down the topic you'll review again tonight.</span></li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  </section>

<script>
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll('.q-check');
  const fill = document.getElementById('meterFill');
  const pct = document.getElementById('meterPct');
  function updateMeter(){
    const total = checks.length;
    const done = document.querySelectorAll('.q-check:checked').length;
    const p = total ? Math.round((done/total)*100) : 0;
    fill.style.width = p + '%';
    pct.textContent = p + '%';
  }
  checks.forEach(c => c.addEventListener('change', updateMeter));
  updateMeter();
</script>

      <p class="lead">A field guide to capacitance and capacitors: what they are, how much charge they can hold, how they behave in circuits, and how much energy they can release. Use this page as your reference while you work through the Activity Sheet.</p>

          <circle class="charge-dot" cx="126" cy="120" r="4" fill="#7A1E12"/>
          <circle class="charge-dot" cx="126" cy="160" r="4" fill="#7A1E12"/>
          <circle class="charge-dot" cx="214" cy="80" r="4" fill="#16324D"/>
          <circle class="charge-dot" cx="214" cy="120" r="4" fill="#16324D"/>
          <circle class="charge-dot" cx="214" cy="160" r="4" fill="#16324D"/>
          <text x="126" y="215" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="12" fill="#E85D4C" text-anchor="middle">+Q</text>
          <text x="214" y="215" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="12" fill="#3E6E9E" text-anchor="middle">−Q</text>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <p class="cap-caption">A charged parallel-plate capacitor: opposite charges on each plate, an electric field between them.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<div class="wrap">
  <div class="toc-mobile">

  </div>

  <div class="layout">
    <aside class="toc">
      <p class="toc-title">On this page</p>
      <a href="#intro"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><rect x="4" y="4" width="3" height="12" fill="#E85D4C"/><rect x="13" y="4" width="3" height="12" fill="#3E6E9E"/></svg> What is a Capacitor?</a>
      <a href="#definition"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="6" fill="none" stroke="#FFB627" stroke-width="2"/></svg> Defining Capacitance</a>
      <a href="#parallel-plate"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><rect x="3" y="5" width="14" height="10" rx="2" fill="none" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="2"/></svg> The Parallel-Plate Formula</a>
      <a href="#series-parallel"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path d="M3 10h14M8 5v10M12 5v10" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="2"/></svg> Series &amp; Parallel</a>
      <a href="#energy"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path d="M11 2 4 12h5l-1 6 7-10h-5z" fill="#FFB627"/></svg> Stored Energy</a>
      <a href="#applications"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7" fill="none" stroke="#4CAF8C" stroke-width="2"/></svg> Real-World Uses</a>
      <a href="#recap"><svg class="toc-ic" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><rect x="3" y="3" width="14" height="14" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/></svg> Formula Recap</a>
    </aside>

    <main class="content">

      <section class="card" id="intro">
        <span class="kicker">01 · Foundations</span>
        <h2>What is a Capacitor?</h2>
        <p>A <strong>capacitor</strong> is a component that stores electric charge — and with it, electric potential energy — inside an electric field. In its simplest form it is two conducting plates placed close together, separated by an insulating material called a <strong>dielectric</strong>.</p>
        <p>When the plates are connected to a voltage source (like a battery), electrons are pushed onto one plate and pulled off the other. One plate ends up with excess positive charge \(+Q\), the other with equal and opposite charge \(-Q\). No charge actually crosses the dielectric — the capacitor stores energy in the electric field that forms between the plates.</p>
      </section>

      <section class="card" id="definition">
        <span class="kicker">02 · The Core Idea</span>
        <h2>Defining Capacitance</h2>
        <p><strong>Capacitance</strong> (\(C\)) tells you how much charge a capacitor can store for every volt of potential difference applied across it. A capacitor with a large capacitance can hold a lot of charge without needing a large voltage.</p>
        <div class="formula-box">
          <div class="mathjax-wrapper">
            \[ C = \frac{Q}{V} \]
          </div>
          <div class="meta">
            <strong>\(Q\)</strong> = charge stored (Coulombs, C)<br>
            <strong>\(V\)</strong> = potential difference (Volts, V)<br>
            <strong>\(C\)</strong> = capacitance (Farads, F)<br><br>
            <em>Common units:</em> \(1 \, \mu\text{F} = 10^{-6} \text{ F}\)
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="card" id="parallel-plate">
        <span class="kicker">03 · Geometry Matters</span>
        <h2>The Parallel-Plate Capacitor</h2>
        <p>For two flat plates facing each other, capacitance depends only on their geometry and the material between them:</p>
        <div class="formula-box">
          <div class="mathjax-wrapper">
            \[ C = \frac{\varepsilon_0 \varepsilon_r A}{d} \]
          </div>
          <div class="meta">
            <strong>\(\varepsilon_0\)</strong> = \( 8.85 \times 10^{-12} \text{ F/m} \)<br>
            <strong>\(\varepsilon_r\)</strong> = dielectric constant (1 for air)<br>
            <strong>\(A\)</strong> = area of one plate (\(\text{m}^2\))<br>
            <strong>\(d\)</strong> = distance between plates (\(\text{m}\))
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="factor-grid">
          <div class="factor">
            <span class="tag">Area (\(A\)) ↑</span>
            <p>Larger overlapping plate <strong>area</strong> gives more room to store charge — capacitance increases.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="factor">
            <span class="tag">Distance (\(d\)) ↓</span>
            <p>Smaller <strong>separation</strong> between plates means a stronger field for the same voltage — capacitance increases.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="factor">
            <span class="tag">Dielectric (\(\varepsilon_r\)) ↑</span>
            <p>A better insulating <strong>dielectric</strong> lets the plates hold more charge at the same voltage.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="card" id="series-parallel">
        <span class="kicker">04 · In Circuits</span>
        <h2>Capacitors in Series and Parallel</h2>
        <p>Just like resistors, capacitors combine differently depending on how they're wired.</p>
        <div class="two-col">
          <div class="panel series">
            <h3>Series</h3>
            <div class="mathjax-panel">
              \[ \frac{1}{C_{\text{total}}} = \frac{1}{C_1} + \frac{1}{C_2} + \dots \]
            </div>
            <div class="shortcut-box">
              <strong>Hint for 2 Capacitors:</strong> Avoid fractions by using the product-over-sum rule:<br>
              \[ C_{\text{total}} = \frac{C_1 \times C_2}{C_1 + C_2} \]
            </div>
            <ul>
              <li>Every capacitor stores the <strong>same charge</strong> (\(Q_1 = Q_2 = Q_{\text{total}}\)).</li>
              <li>The total voltage splits across each capacitor.</li>
              <li>Total capacitance is always <strong>smaller</strong> than the smallest individual \(C\).</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="panel parallel">
            <h3>Parallel</h3>
            <div class="mathjax-panel">
              \[ C_{\text{total}} = C_1 + C_2 + \dots \]
            </div>
            <ul>
              <li>Every capacitor feels the <strong>same voltage</strong> (\(V_1 = V_2 = V_{\text{total}}\)).</li>
              <li>The total charge splits across each capacitor.</li>
              <li>Total capacitance is always <strong>larger</strong> than any individual \(C\).</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="card" id="energy">
        <span class="kicker">05 · Storing Energy</span>
        <h2>Energy Stored in a Capacitor</h2>
        <p>Charging a capacitor takes work, because each new bit of charge has to be pushed against the charge already sitting on the plates. That work is stored as electric potential energy:</p>
        <div class="formula-box">
          <div class="mathjax-wrapper">
             \[ U = \frac{1}{2}QV = \frac{1}{2}CV^2 = \frac{Q^2}{2C} \]
          </div>
          <div class="meta">
            Any of the three forms works — pick whichever variables you already know based on the question. Energy \(U\) is measured in Joules (J).
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="card" id="applications">
        <span class="kicker">06 · Why It Matters</span>
        <h2>Where You'll Find Capacitors</h2>
        <div class="app-grid">
          <div class="app-item">
            <svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 2 5 13h6l-1 9 8-13h-6z" fill="#FFB627"/></svg>
            <div><h4>Camera flash</h4><p>Charges slowly from a small battery, then releases all its energy in a fraction of a second for a bright flash.</p></div>
          </div>
          <div class="app-item">
            <svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 21s-7-4.4-9.5-8.6C.7 8.8 2.3 5 6 5c2 0 3.3 1.1 4 2.2C10.7 6.1 12 5 14 5c3.7 0 5.3 3.8 3.5 7.4C19 16.6 12 21 12 21z" fill="#E85D4C"/></svg>
            <div><h4>Defibrillators</h4><p>Store energy over a few seconds, then deliver a large, controlled pulse to the heart much faster than a battery ever could alone.</p></div>
          </div>
          <div class="app-item">
            <svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2" fill="none" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="2"/><rect x="7" y="9" width="4" height="4" fill="#3E6E9E"/><rect x="13" y="9" width="4" height="4" fill="#3E6E9E"/></svg>
            <div><h4>Memory (DRAM)</h4><p>Each bit of data in computer memory is stored as a tiny charge on a microscopic capacitor.</p></div>
          </div>
          <div class="app-item">
            <svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 12h4l2-6 4 12 2-6h4" fill="none" stroke="#4CAF8C" stroke-width="2"/></svg>
            <div><h4>Smoothing &amp; touchscreens</h4><p>Smooth out bumpy power-supply voltages, and detect the tiny change in capacitance created by your finger on a touchscreen.</p></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="card recap" id="recap">
        <span class="kicker">Quick Reference</span>
        <h2>Formula Recap</h2>
        <div class="recap-grid">
          <div class="recap-item"><div class="mathjax-recap">\( C = \frac{Q}{V} \)</div><p>Definition</p></div>
          <div class="recap-item"><div class="mathjax-recap">\( C = \frac{\varepsilon_0\varepsilon_r A}{d} \)</div><p>Parallel-plate</p></div>
          <div class="recap-item"><div class="mathjax-recap">\( \frac{1}{C_t} = \Sigma \frac{1}{C_i} \)</div><p>Series</p></div>
          <div class="recap-item"><div class="mathjax-recap">\( C_t = \Sigma C_i \)</div><p>Parallel</p></div>
          <div class="recap-item"><div class="mathjax-recap">\( U = \frac{1}{2}CV^2 \)</div><p>Stored energy</p></div>
          <div class="recap-item"><div class="mathjax-recap">\( U = \frac{Q^2}{2C} \)</div><p>Stored energy (alt)</p></div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="cta-banner">
        <div>
          <h3>Ready to put this into practice?</h3>
          <p>Head to the Activity Sheet — a 75-minute worksheet you'll answer in your notebook.</p>
        </div>
        <a class="btn btn-primary" href="activity.html">Open Activity Sheet →</a>
      </section>

    </main>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
  FQ.renderHUD('#hudMount', {});

  // starfield
  const sf = document.getElementById('stars');
  for(let i=0;i<50;i++){
    const s = document.createElement('span');
    s.style.left = Math.random()*100+'vw';
    s.style.top = Math.random()*100+'vh';
    s.style.animationDelay = (Math.random()*3)+'s';
    sf.appendChild(s);
  }

  const progress = FQ.get();
  const road = document.getElementById('mapRoad');
  road.innerHTML = FQ.STAGES.map((s,i)=>{
    const unlocked = FQ.isUnlocked(s.id);
    const done = progress[s.id] && progress[s.id].boss && progress[s.id].boss.done;
    const stars = FQ.stageStars(s.id);
    const side = i%2===0 ? '' : 'right';
    const cls = [unlocked?'':'locked', done?'done':''].join(' ');
    return `
      <a class="node ${side} ${cls}" href="${unlocked ? s.file : '#'}">
        <div class="badge">${s.icon}</div>
        <div class="info">
          <h3>${s.num}. ${s.title}</h3>
          <div class="tag">${s.tag}</div>
          <div class="stars">${unlocked ? '★'.repeat(stars)+'☆'.repeat(3-stars) : '🔒 Terkunci'}</div>
        </div>
      </a>`;
  }).join('');

  document.querySelectorAll('.node').forEach(n=>n.addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
    if(n.classList.contains('locked')){ e.preventDefault(); FQ.toast('Kalahkan bos wilayah sebelumnya dulu!'); FQ.sfx.wrong(); }
    else FQ.sfx.click();
  }));

  const total = FQ.totalStars();
  document.getElementById('statsPanel').innerHTML = `
    <h2 style="margin-top:0;">📊 Progresmu</h2>
    <p>Total Bintang: <b style="color:var(--gold);">${total} / 18</b></p>
    <div class="hpbar player" style="max-width:100%;"><span style="width:${(total/18*100).toFixed(0)}%"></span></div>
  `;

  document.getElementById('resetBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    if(confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus semua progres di perangkat ini?')) FQ.resetAll();
  });
</script>

      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">🗺️ Peta Konsep Elektrostatika</h2>
      <p class="small">Begini semua materi yang sudah kamu taklukkan saling berhubungan:</p>
      <div class="visual-box" style="min-height:220px;">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 700 160" width="100%" style="max-width:700px;">
          <defs><marker id="pm" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="4" refY="4" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 Z" fill="#f6b93b"/></marker></defs>

          </g>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <p class="small">Muatan menghasilkan <b>gaya</b> (Hukum Coulomb) yang membentuk <b>medan</b> di sekitarnya (Hukum Gauss membantu menghitungnya untuk bentuk simetris). Medan ini terkait dengan <b>potensial</b> (skalar), yang menentukan <b>energi</b> saat muatan berpindah. Kapasitor memanfaatkan konsep ini untuk menyimpan muatan.</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- FLASHCARD -->

      <div class="grid-2" id="flashGrid"></div>

  <!-- STUDI KASUS -->
  <div class="tabpane" id="pane-kasus">
    <div class="pixel-panel">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">📘 Studi Kasus 1: Dua Muatan</h2>
      <p>Dua muatan q₁ = 2×10⁻⁶ C dan q₂ = 8×10⁻⁶ C terpisah sejauh r = 0,4 m. Hitung gaya Coulomb dan medan listrik akibat q₁ di lokasi q₂.</p>
      <button class="pixel-btn cyan" id="case1btn" style="font-size:.6rem;">Tunjukkan Langkah 1</button>
      <div class="case-step" id="case1-1"><b>Langkah 1 — Gaya Coulomb:</b><br>F = k·q₁q₂/r² = 9×10⁹ × (2×10⁻⁶ × 8×10⁻⁶) / (0,4)² = 9×10⁹ × 1,6×10⁻¹¹ / 0,16 = <b>0,9 N</b></div>
      <button class="pixel-btn cyan" id="case1btn2" style="font-size:.6rem; margin-top:.6rem;">Tunjukkan Langkah 2</button>
      <div class="case-step" id="case1-2"><b>Langkah 2 — Medan akibat q₁ di posisi q₂:</b><br>E = k·q₁/r² = 9×10⁹ × 2×10⁻⁶ / 0,16 = <b>1,125×10⁵ N/C</b></div>

      <h2 style="margin-top:1.6rem;">📗 Studi Kasus 2: Kapasitor Seri</h2>
      <p>Kapasitor C₁ = 6 μF dan C₂ = 3 μF disusun seri, dihubungkan sumber tegangan 12 V. Hitung kapasitas total dan muatan totalnya.</p>
      <button class="pixel-btn cyan" id="case2btn" style="font-size:.6rem;">Tunjukkan Langkah 1</button>
      <div class="case-step" id="case2-1"><b>Langkah 1 — Kapasitas total:</b><br>1/Cs = 1/6 + 1/3 = 1/6 + 2/6 = 3/6 = 1/2 → <b>Cs = 2 μF</b></div>
      <button class="pixel-btn cyan" id="case2btn2" style="font-size:.6rem; margin-top:.6rem;">Tunjukkan Langkah 2</button>
      <div class="case-step" id="case2-2"><b>Langkah 2 — Muatan total (q = Cs·V):</b><br>q = 2 μF × 12 V = <b>24 μC</b> (ingat: pada rangkaian seri, muatan ini sama di C₁ maupun C₂)</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- BOSS AKHIR -->
  <div class="tabpane" id="pane-boss">
    <div class="pixel-panel tone-magenta">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">👑 Bos Akhir: Sang Penakluk Elektrostatika</h2>
      <p class="small">Gabungan seluruh materi Wilayah 1–5. Ini tantangan terakhirmu!</p>
      <div id="bossBox"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  { q:'Dua muatan sejenis (− dan −) saling didekatkan. Sifat gayanya adalah...', weight:12,
    options:['Tarik-menarik','Tolak-menolak','Netral'], answer:1, explain:'Muatan sejenis selalu tolak-menolak.' },

    options:['1/9 kali','1/3 kali','3 kali','9 kali'], answer:0, explain:'F∝1/r² → 1/3²=1/9.' },
  { q:'Dua medan listrik tegak lurus 9 N/C dan 12 N/C. Resultannya adalah...', weight:15,
    options:['21 N/C','15 N/C','3 N/C','108 N/C'], answer:1, explain:'√(9²+12²)=√(81+144)=√225=15 N/C.' },
  { q:'Kuat medan listrik di dalam bola konduktor berongga bermuatan adalah...', weight:10,
    options:['Sama dengan permukaan','Nol','Tak hingga'], answer:1, explain:'Selalu nol di dalam konduktor.' },
  { q:'Potensial listrik adalah besaran...', weight:10,
    options:['Vektor','Skalar'], answer:1, explain:'V adalah skalar.' },

    options:['20 J','11 J','32 J','5 J'], answer:0, explain:'W=q(V₂−V₁)=4×(8−3)=4×5=20 J.' },
  { q:'Dua kapasitor 4 μF disusun paralel, kapasitas totalnya adalah...', weight:10,
    options:['2 μF','4 μF','8 μF','1/8 μF'], answer:2, explain:'Cp=4+4=8 μF.' },
  { q:'Dua muatan q₁=5×10⁻⁶ C dan q₂=4×10⁻⁶ C berjarak r=0,3 m (k=9×10⁹). Berapa gaya Coulombnya?', weight:15,
    options:['2 N','20 N','0,2 N','200 N'], answer:0, explain:'F=9×10⁹×(5×10⁻⁶×4×10⁻⁶)/(0,3)²=9×10⁹×2×10⁻¹¹/0,09=2 N.' },
];

Saat jarak <span class="formula">r</span> menjadi 2 kali lebih jauh, "kekuatan" itu tersebar di permukaan yang luasnya 4 kali lebih besar — sehingga yang diterima di satu titik menjadi <b>¼ kalinya</b>.</p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 300 140" width="300">
            <circle cx="60" cy="70" r="10" fill="#f6b93b"/>

            <circle cx="200" cy="70" r="10" fill="#f6b93b"/>

            <text x="281" y="112" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#3be8e0">2r</text>
            <text x="281" y="35" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#f3ead2">¼ gaya (area 4×)</text>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <p><b>Pola pentingnya:</b></p>
        <ul>
          <li>Jarak menjadi <span class="formula">2×</span> → gaya menjadi <span class="formula">¼×</span></li>
          <li>Jarak menjadi <span class="formula">3×</span> → gaya menjadi <span class="formula">1/9×</span></li>
          <li>Jarak menjadi <span class="formula">½×</span> (lebih dekat) → gaya menjadi <span class="formula">4×</span></li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="concept-card" data-i="2">

        <p class="small center">dengan <span class="formula">k = 9×10⁹ N·m²/C²</span>, <span class="formula">q₁, q₂</span> = besar muatan (Coulomb), <span class="formula">r</span> = jarak antar muatan (meter).</p>

        <p>Saat lebih dari dua muatan segaris (satu dimensi), gaya total pada satu muatan adalah <b>jumlah aljabar</b> (dengan tanda arah) dari gaya akibat tiap muatan lain — bukan sekadar dijumlah begitu saja.</p>
        <div class="visual-box">

            <text x="250" y="70" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#f3ead2">C (+)</text>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <p class="small">Contoh: gaya total pada <b>B</b> = (tarikan dari A ke kiri) + (tarikan dari C ke kanan). Karena berlawanan arah, hasilnya adalah <b>selisih</b> keduanya, arahnya mengikuti gaya yang lebih besar.</p>

<script>
const STAGE_ID = 'stage1';
FQ.renderHUD('#hudMount', { backHref:'index.html' });

/* ---------------- Tabs ---------------- */
const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#tabbar button');
tabs.forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    FQ.sfx.click();
    tabs.forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tabpane').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('pane-'+btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
  });
});
function markTabDone(tab){
  const b = document.querySelector(`#tabbar button[data-tab="${tab}"]`);
  if(b && !b.querySelector('.check')) b.innerHTML += ' <span class="check">✔</span>';
}
const prog = FQ.get()[STAGE_ID];
if(prog.math) markTabDone('math');
if(prog.latihan.done) markTabDone('latihan');
if(prog.boss.done) markTabDone('boss');

/* ---------------- Math warmup ---------------- */
const warmupItems = [
  { q:'Jika jarak antar dua muatan dijadikan <b>2 kali</b> lebih jauh, gaya Coulomb menjadi berapa kali semula?',
    options:['2 kali','4 kali','¼ kali','½ kali'], answer:2,
    hint:'Gunakan F ∝ 1/r². Jika r → 2r, maka F → 1/(2)² = 1/4.',
    explain:'F baru = 1/(2²) = 1/4 kali gaya semula.' },
  { q:'Jika jarak diperkecil menjadi <b>1/3</b> kali semula, gaya Coulomb menjadi berapa kali?',
    options:['3 kali','1/3 kali','9 kali','1/9 kali'], answer:2,
    hint:'r → r/3, maka F → 1/(1/3)² = 9.',
    explain:'F baru = 1/(1/3)² = 9 kali gaya semula.' },
  { q:'Diketahui F = k·q₁q₂/r² dengan k=9×10⁹, q₁=q₂=2×10⁻⁶ C, r=0,1 m. Berapa F (dalam Newton)? <span class="small">(bulatkan)</span>',
    type:'fill', answer:3.6, tol:1,
    hint:'F = 9×10⁹ × (2×10⁻⁶ × 2×10⁻⁶) / (0,1)² = 9×10⁹ × 4×10⁻¹² / 0,01',
    explain:'F = 9×10⁹ × 4×10⁻¹² / 0,01 = 3,6 N.' },
  { q:'Sebuah gaya F terjadi pada jarak r. Jika jarak menjadi 4 kali lebih jauh, gaya menjadi...',
    options:['1/16 kali','1/4 kali','4 kali','16 kali'], answer:0,
    hint:'F ∝ 1/r², maka 1/(4)² = 1/16.',
    explain:'F baru = 1/16 kali gaya semula — melemah drastis karena kuadrat.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderMathWarmup(document.getElementById('warmupBox'), warmupItems, {
  onDone(correct,total){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'math', true);
    markTabDone('math');
    FQ.toast(`Pemanasan selesai! Benar ${correct}/${total} 🔥`);
  }
});

/* ---------------- Concept cards ---------------- */
const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.concept-card');
let cardIdx = 0;
function showCard(i){
  cardIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(cards.length-1, i));
  cards.forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('active'));
  cards[cardIdx].classList.add('active');
  [document.getElementById('dots'), document.getElementById('dots2')].forEach(d=>{
    d.innerHTML = cards.length ? Array.from(cards).map((_,j)=>`<span class="${j===cardIdx?'on':''}"></span>`).join('') : '';
  });
  document.getElementById('prevCard').disabled = cardIdx===0;
  if(cardIdx===cards.length-1){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'materi', true);
  }
}
document.getElementById('prevCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{ FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx-1); });
document.getElementById('nextCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{ FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx+1); });
showCard(0);

/* ---------------- Coulomb simulator ---------------- */
const q1El=document.getElementById('simQ1'), q2El=document.getElementById('simQ2'), rEl=document.getElementById('simR');
function updateSim(){
  const q1 = +q1El.value, q2 = +q2El.value, r = +rEl.value;
  document.getElementById('simQ1val').textContent = ' '+q1+' µC';
  document.getElementById('simQ2val').textContent = ' '+q2+' µC';
  document.getElementById('simRval').textContent = ' '+r+' cm';

  const trackW = 380;
  const rpx = 40 + (r-2)/(20-2)*260;
  const xA = (trackW-rpx)/2 - 20, xB = xA + rpx;
  const a = document.getElementById('chargeA'), b = document.getElementById('chargeB');
  a.style.left = xA+'px'; b.style.left = xB+'px';
  a.style.background = q1>=0 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#5b8dff';
  b.style.background = q2>=0 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#5b8dff';
  a.textContent = q1>=0?'+':'−';
  b.textContent = q2>=0?'+':'−';

  const F = 9e9 * Math.abs(q1*1e-6) * Math.abs(q2*1e-6) / Math.pow(r/100,2);
  const attract = (q1>=0) !== (q2>=0);
  const arrowA = document.getElementById('arrowA'), arrowB = document.getElementById('arrowB');
  const scale = Math.min(1.6, 0.5 + Math.log10(F+1)/3);
  arrowA.style.fontSize = (scale)+'rem';
  arrowB.style.fontSize = (scale)+'rem';
  if(attract){
    arrowA.textContent='→'; arrowB.textContent='←';
    arrowA.style.left = (xA+20)+'px'; arrowB.style.left=(xB-20)+'px';
    arrowA.style.color = arrowB.style.color = 'var(--success)';
  } else {
    arrowA.textContent='←'; arrowB.textContent='→';
    arrowA.style.left = (xA-20)+'px'; arrowB.style.left=(xB+40)+'px';
    arrowA.style.color = arrowB.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
  }
  document.getElementById('simF').textContent =
    `F ≈ ${F.toFixed(2)} N — ${attract? 'TARIK-MENARIK':'TOLAK-MENOLAK'} (q berlawanan jenis: ${attract?'ya':'tidak'})`;
}
[q1El,q2El,rEl].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('input', updateSim));
updateSim();

/* ---------------- Practice ---------------- */
const practiceItems = [
  { q:'Dua muatan sejenis (+ dan +) didekatkan. Apa yang terjadi?', options:['Tarik-menarik','Tolak-menolak','Tidak ada gaya','Bergabung menjadi netral'], answer:1, explain:'Muatan sejenis selalu tolak-menolak.' },
  { q:'Satuan gaya Coulomb (F) dalam Sistem Internasional adalah...', options:['Coulomb (C)','Newton (N)','Joule (J)','Volt (V)'], answer:1, explain:'F adalah gaya, satuannya Newton.' },
  { q:'Jika jarak antar muatan diperbesar 5 kali, gaya Coulomb menjadi...', options:['1/25 kali','1/5 kali','5 kali','25 kali'], answer:0, explain:'F ∝ 1/r² → 1/5² = 1/25.' },
  { q:'Dua muatan q₁ = 3 µC dan q₂ = −6 µC berjarak 3 cm. Sifat gayanya adalah...', options:['Tolak-menolak','Tarik-menarik','Netral (F=0)','Tidak bisa ditentukan'], answer:1, explain:'Beda jenis (+ dan −) selalu tarik-menarik.' },
  { q:'Pada tiga muatan segaris A(+), B(−), C(+) dengan B di tengah, gaya total pada B berasal dari...', options:['Hanya muatan A','Hanya muatan C','Jumlah aljabar gaya dari A dan C','Rata-rata gaya A dan C'], answer:2, explain:'Gaya total = jumlah aljabar (memperhatikan arah) dari semua gaya yang bekerja pada B.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderPractice(document.getElementById('practiceBox'), practiceItems, {
  onDone(correct,total){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'latihan', {done:true, score:correct});
    markTabDone('latihan');
    FQ.toast(`Latihan selesai! Skor ${correct}/${total}`);
  }
});

/* ---------------- Boss ---------------- */
const bossSteps = [
  { q:'Dua muatan positif saling didekatkan. Ke arah mana gaya yang dialami tiap muatan?', weight:20,
    options:['Saling menjauh (tolak-menolak)','Saling mendekat (tarik-menarik)','Diam, tidak ada gaya'], answer:0,
    explain:'Muatan sejenis (+ dan +) selalu tolak-menolak.' },
  { q:'Mula-mula gaya antar dua muatan adalah F. Jika jaraknya dijadikan setengah kali semula, gaya yang baru adalah...', weight:30,
    options:['F/4','F/2','2F','4F'], answer:3,
    explain:'r→r/2 maka F baru = 1/(1/2)² = 4F.' },
  { q:'Dua muatan q₁ = 4×10⁻⁶ C dan q₂ = 5×10⁻⁶ C berjarak r = 2×10⁻¹ m (k=9×10⁹). Berapa besar gaya Coulomb-nya?', weight:50,
    options:['0,45 N','4,5 N','45 N','450 N'], answer:1,
    explain:'F = 9×10⁹ × (4×10⁻⁶ × 5×10⁻⁶)/(0,2)² = 9×10⁹×2×10⁻¹¹/0,04 = 4,5 N.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderBoss(document.getElementById('bossBox'), bossSteps, {
  bossName:'Penjaga Elektron', bossEmoji:'👾',
  onWin(){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'boss', {done:true, stars:3});
    markTabDone('boss');
    FQ.sfx.unlock();
    setTimeout(()=>FQ.toast('Wilayah 2: Hutan Vektor telah terbuka! 🌲'), 800);
  }
});
</script>

    <div class="pixel-panel">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">⚡ Gaya &amp; Medan</h2>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">F = k · q₁q₂ / r²</p>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">E = k · q / r²</p>
      <p class="small">k = 9×10⁹ N·m²/C²</p>
    </div>
    <div class="pixel-panel tone-cyan">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">🕳️ Gauss</h2>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">φ = E·A·cosθ = q/ε₀</p>
      <p class="small">ε₀ = 8,85×10⁻¹² C²/N·m²</p>
      <p class="small">Di dalam konduktor berongga: <b>E = 0</b></p>
    </div>
    <div class="pixel-panel">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">⛰️ Potensial &amp; Energi</h2>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">V = k · q / r</p>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">W = q(V₂ − V₁)</p>
      <p class="small">V dan Eₚ adalah besaran <b>skalar</b>.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="pixel-panel tone-magenta">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">🏰 Kapasitor</h2>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">C = q/V</p>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">W = ½CV²</p>
      <p class="formula center" style="display:block; text-align:center;">C = ε₀A/d</p>
      <p class="small">Seri: 1/Cs = 1/C₁+1/C₂+... &nbsp;|&nbsp; Paralel: Cp = C₁+C₂+...</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="pixel-panel" style="margin-top:1.2rem;">
    <h2 style="margin-top:0;">📐 Tabel Trigonometri Sudut Istimewa</h2>
    <table class="ref">
      <tr><th>Sudut</th><th>0°</th><th>30°</th><th>37°</th><th>45°</th><th>53°</th><th>60°</th><th>90°</th></tr>
      <tr><td>sin</td><td>0</td><td>1/2</td><td>3/5</td><td>½√2</td><td>4/5</td><td>½√3</td><td>1</td></tr>
      <tr><td>cos</td><td>1</td><td>½√3</td><td>4/5</td><td>½√2</td><td>3/5</td><td>1/2</td><td>0</td></tr>
      <tr><td>tan</td><td>0</td><td>⅓√3</td><td>3/4</td><td>1</td><td>4/3</td><td>√3</td><td>−</td></tr>
    </table>

<script>
const STAGE_ID = 'stage3';
FQ.renderHUD('#hudMount', { backHref:'index.html' });
if(!FQ.isUnlocked(STAGE_ID)){
  document.querySelector('.wrap').innerHTML = `<div class="locked-note pixel-panel"><h2>🔒 Wilayah Terkunci</h2><p>Selesaikan dulu Wilayah 2: Hutan Vektor.</p><a href="index.html" class="pixel-btn cyan">Kembali ke Peta</a></div>`;
  throw new Error('locked');
}

const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#tabbar button');
tabs.forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  FQ.sfx.click();
  tabs.forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tabpane').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('pane-'+btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
}));
function markTabDone(tab){
  const b = document.querySelector(`#tabbar button[data-tab="${tab}"]`);
  if(b && !b.querySelector('.check')) b.innerHTML += ' <span class="check">✔</span>';
}
const prog = FQ.get()[STAGE_ID];
if(prog.math) markTabDone('math');
if(prog.latihan.done) markTabDone('latihan');
if(prog.boss.done) markTabDone('boss');

const warmupItems = [
  { q:'Luas permukaan bola dengan jari-jari r dirumuskan sebagai...', options:['πr²','4πr²','2πr','πd'], answer:1,
    hint:'Rumus luas permukaan bola: A = 4πr².', explain:'A = 4πr².' },
  { q:'Berapa nilai cos 0°?', options:['0','1','−1','0,5'], answer:1, hint:'Saat sudut antara medan dan garis normal permukaan 0° (tegak lurus permukaan), fluks maksimum.', explain:'cos 0° = 1 (fluks maksimum saat medan tegak lurus permukaan).' },
  { q:'Berapa nilai cos 90°?', options:['0','1','−1','∞'], answer:0, hint:'Saat medan sejajar permukaan (tidak menembusnya), fluksnya nol.', explain:'cos 90° = 0 (medan sejajar permukaan → fluks nol).' },
  { q:'Jika E = 200 N/C, A = 0,5 m², dan θ = 0°, berapa fluks φ = E·A·cosθ?', type:'fill', answer:100, tol:1,
    hint:'φ = 200 × 0,5 × cos 0° = 200 × 0,5 × 1', explain:'φ = 200 × 0,5 × 1 = 100 N·m²/C.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderMathWarmup(document.getElementById('warmupBox'), warmupItems, {
  onDone(c,t){ FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'math',true); markTabDone('math'); FQ.toast(`Pemanasan selesai! ${c}/${t}`); }
});

const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.concept-card');
let cardIdx=0;
function showCard(i){
  cardIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(cards.length-1,i));
  cards.forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('active'));
  cards[cardIdx].classList.add('active');
  [document.getElementById('dots'),document.getElementById('dots2')].forEach(d=>{
    d.innerHTML = Array.from(cards).map((_,j)=>`<span class="${j===cardIdx?'on':''}"></span>`).join('');
  });
  document.getElementById('prevCard').disabled = cardIdx===0;
  if(cardIdx===cards.length-1) FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'materi',true);
}
document.getElementById('prevCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx-1);});
document.getElementById('nextCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx+1);});
showCard(0);

const practiceItems = [
  { q:'Kuat medan listrik di dalam bola konduktor berongga (r < R) adalah...', options:['Sama dengan di permukaan','Selalu nol','Tak terhingga','Tergantung muatan luar'], answer:1, explain:'Di dalam konduktor, E selalu 0 karena muatan hanya berada di permukaan.' },
  { q:'Fluks listrik dirumuskan sebagai...', options:['φ = E/A','φ = E·A·cosθ','φ = E+A','φ = E·A²'], answer:1, explain:'φ = E·A·cosθ, sesuai Hukum Gauss.' },
  { q:'Medan listrik di antara dua keping sejajar bermuatan berlawanan bersifat...', options:['Homogen (seragam)','Selalu nol','Melingkar','Acak'], answer:0, explain:'Medan di antara keping sejajar bersifat homogen: besar dan arah sama di semua titik.' },
  { q:'Jika medan listrik sejajar dengan permukaan (θ=90°), fluks yang menembus permukaan itu adalah...', options:['Maksimum','Nol','Setengah maksimum','Tak hingga'], answer:1, explain:'cos 90° = 0, sehingga fluks = 0 (tidak ada garis medan yang menembus).' },
  { q:'Muatan pada konduktor berongga bermuatan tersebar di...', options:['Pusat bola','Merata di seluruh volume','Permukaan luar','Tidak tersebar'], answer:2, explain:'Muatan bebas pada konduktor selalu bergerak ke permukaan luar hingga setimbang.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderPractice(document.getElementById('practiceBox'), practiceItems, {
  onDone(c,t){ FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'latihan',{done:true,score:c}); markTabDone('latihan'); FQ.toast(`Latihan selesai! Skor ${c}/${t}`); }
});

const bossSteps = [
  { q:'Di dalam sebuah bola konduktor berongga bermuatan, berapa kuat medan listriknya?', weight:20,
    options:['Sama dengan di luar bola','Nol','Tak terhingga','Setengah dari permukaan'], answer:1,
    explain:'Medan listrik di dalam konduktor selalu nol.' },
  { q:'Medan listrik E=300 N/C menembus tegak lurus (θ=0°) suatu bidang seluas A=0,2 m². Berapa fluksnya?', weight:30,
    options:['1500','60','300','1,5'], answer:1,
    explain:'φ = E·A·cos0° = 300×0,2×1 = 60 N·m²/C.' },
  { q:'Bidang seluas 0,4 m² ditembus medan E=250 N/C dengan sudut 90° terhadap bidang (medan sejajar bidang). Berapa fluksnya?', weight:50,
    options:['100','0','25','250'], answer:1,
    explain:'θ=90° berarti medan sejajar bidang → cos 90°=0 → φ=0.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderBoss(document.getElementById('bossBox'), bossSteps, {
  bossName:'Naga Fluks', bossEmoji:'🐉',
  onWin(){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'boss',{done:true,stars:3});
    markTabDone('boss'); FQ.sfx.unlock();
    setTimeout(()=>FQ.toast('Wilayah 4: Bukit Potensial telah terbuka! ⛰️'), 800);
  }
});

    <div class="pixel-panel tone-cyan">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">🥋 Pemanasan: Trigonometri &amp; Pythagoras</h2>
      <p>Saat dua gaya/medan bekerja tegak lurus, kita menggabungkannya seperti dua sisi segitiga siku-siku. Kuasai dulu dua alat bantu ini:</p>
      <table class="ref" style="margin:1rem 0;">
        <tr><th>Sudut</th><th>0°</th><th>30°</th><th>37°</th><th>45°</th><th>53°</th><th>60°</th><th>90°</th></tr>
        <tr><td>sin</td><td>0</td><td>1/2</td><td>3/5</td><td>½√2</td><td>4/5</td><td>½√3</td><td>1</td></tr>
        <tr><td>cos</td><td>1</td><td>½√3</td><td>4/5</td><td>½√2</td><td>3/5</td><td>1/2</td><td>0</td></tr>
      </table>
      <div id="warmupBox"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="tabpane" id="pane-materi">
    <div class="pixel-panel">
      <div class="dots" id="dots"></div>

      <div class="concept-card active" data-i="0">
        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">① Menggabungkan Dua Arah Tegak Lurus</h2>
        <p>Jika dua vektor gaya/medan saling tegak lurus (membentuk sudut 90°), besar resultannya dihitung dengan <b>Teorema Pythagoras</b>:</p>
        <p class="center"><span class="formula">R = √(A² + B²)</span></p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 220 160" width="220">
            <line x1="30" y1="130" x2="170" y2="130" stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrow2)"/>
            <line x1="30" y1="130" x2="30" y2="30" stroke="#f6b93b" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrow2)"/>
            <line x1="170" y1="130" x2="30" y2="30" stroke="#ff5d8f" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="5,4" marker-end="url(#arrow2)"/>
            <text x="100" y="148" fill="#3be8e0" font-size="12">A</text>
            <text x="10" y="80" fill="#f6b93b" font-size="12">B</text>
            <text x="90" y="70" fill="#ff5d8f" font-size="12">R</text>
            <defs><marker id="arrow2" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="4" refY="4" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 Z" fill="#f3ead2"/></marker></defs>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <p class="small">Ini sama persis dengan mencari sisi miring segitiga siku-siku — hanya sekarang sisi-sisinya adalah gaya atau medan listrik.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="concept-card" data-i="1">
        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">② Apa itu Medan Listrik?</h2>
        <p>Medan listrik (<span class="formula">E</span>) adalah "ruang pengaruh" di sekitar muatan. Digambarkan dengan garis gaya yang <b>keluar dari muatan positif</b> dan <b>masuk ke muatan negatif</b> — persis seperti pola serbuk besi di sekitar magnet.</p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 260 140" width="260">
            <circle cx="70" cy="70" r="16" fill="#ff6b6b"/><text x="70" y="75" text-anchor="middle" fill="#120c1f" font-weight="bold">+</text>
            <g stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none" opacity=".8">
              <line x1="86" y1="70" x2="140" y2="70" marker-end="url(#arrow3)"/>
              <line x1="70" y1="54" x2="70" y2="10" marker-end="url(#arrow3)" transform="rotate(35 70 70)"/>
              <line x1="70" y1="54" x2="70" y2="10" marker-end="url(#arrow3)" transform="rotate(-35 70 70)"/>
              <line x1="70" y1="54" x2="70" y2="10" marker-end="url(#arrow3)" transform="rotate(145 70 70)"/>
              <line x1="70" y1="54" x2="70" y2="10" marker-end="url(#arrow3)" transform="rotate(215 70 70)"/>
            </g>
            <circle cx="200" cy="70" r="16" fill="#5b8dff"/><text x="200" y="75" text-anchor="middle" fill="#120c1f" font-weight="bold">−</text>
            <g stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none" opacity=".8">
              <line x1="252" y1="70" x2="216" y2="70" marker-end="url(#arrow3)"/>
            </g>
            <defs><marker id="arrow3" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" refX="3.5" refY="3.5" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L7,3.5 L0,7 Z" fill="#3be8e0"/></marker></defs>
          </svg>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="concept-card" data-i="2">
        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">③ Aturan Garis Gaya</h2>
        <p>Garis gaya listrik adalah garis khayal yang <b>tidak pernah saling berpotongan</b>. Di antara muatan + dan −, garis-garisnya melengkung mulus dari + menuju −.</p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 260 140" width="260">
            <circle cx="70" cy="70" r="16" fill="#ff6b6b"/><text x="70" y="75" text-anchor="middle" fill="#120c1f" font-weight="bold">+</text>
            <circle cx="190" cy="70" r="16" fill="#5b8dff"/><text x="190" y="75" text-anchor="middle" fill="#120c1f" font-weight="bold">−</text>
            <path d="M 86 60 Q 130 20 174 60" stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none" marker-end="url(#arrow3)"/>
            <path d="M 86 70 Q 130 70 174 70" stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none" marker-end="url(#arrow3)"/>
            <path d="M 86 80 Q 130 120 174 80" stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none" marker-end="url(#arrow3)"/>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <p class="small">Kepadatan garis gaya juga menunjukkan kekuatan medan: makin rapat garis, makin kuat medannya.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="concept-card" data-i="3">
        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">④ Rumus &amp; Resultan Medan (E)</h2>
        <p class="center"><span class="formula" style="font-size:1.1rem;">E = k <span class="frac"><span class="num">q</span><span class="den">r²</span></span></span></p>
        <p>Jika dua sumber muatan menghasilkan medan <span class="formula">E₁</span> dan <span class="formula">E₂</span> yang saling tegak lurus di suatu titik, resultannya:</p>
        <p class="center"><span class="formula">E_total = √(E₁² + E₂²)</span></p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 200 150" width="200">
            <line x1="30" y1="120" x2="150" y2="120" stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrow2b)"/>
            <line x1="30" y1="120" x2="30" y2="30" stroke="#f6b93b" stroke-width="3" marker-end="url(#arrow2b)"/>
            <path d="M 30 105 A 15 15 0 0 1 45 120" fill="none" stroke="#f3ead2" stroke-width="1.5"/>
            <text x="90" y="138" fill="#3be8e0" font-size="11">E₁</text>
            <text x="8" y="80" fill="#f6b93b" font-size="11">E₂</text>
            <defs><marker id="arrow2b" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="4" refY="4" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 Z" fill="#f3ead2"/></marker></defs>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <p class="small">Sudut resultannya bisa dicari dengan <span class="formula">tan θ = E₂/E₁</span> — tapi untuk kelas ini kita fokus dulu ke besarnya saja.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="card-nav">
        <button class="pixel-btn ghost" id="prevCard" style="font-size:.6rem;">← Sebelumnya</button>
        <div class="dots" id="dots2"></div>
        <button class="pixel-btn cyan" id="nextCard" style="font-size:.6rem;">Selanjutnya →</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="tabpane" id="pane-latihan">
    <div class="pixel-panel">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">📝 Latihan Konsep</h2>
      <div id="practiceBox"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="tabpane" id="pane-boss">
    <div class="pixel-panel tone-magenta">
      <h2 style="margin-top:0;">👾 Pertempuran Bos: Roh Medan</h2>
      <div id="bossBox"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="center" style="margin-top:2rem;">
    <a href="index.html" class="pixel-btn ghost">← Kembali ke Peta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
const STAGE_ID = 'stage2';
FQ.renderHUD('#hudMount', { backHref:'index.html' });
if(!FQ.isUnlocked(STAGE_ID)){
  document.querySelector('.wrap').innerHTML = `<div class="locked-note pixel-panel"><h2>🔒 Wilayah Terkunci</h2><p>Selesaikan dulu Wilayah 1: Desa Coulomb.</p><a href="index.html" class="pixel-btn cyan">Kembali ke Peta</a></div>`;
  throw new Error('locked');
}

const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#tabbar button');
tabs.forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  FQ.sfx.click();
  tabs.forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tabpane').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('pane-'+btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
}));
function markTabDone(tab){
  const b = document.querySelector(`#tabbar button[data-tab="${tab}"]`);
  if(b && !b.querySelector('.check')) b.innerHTML += ' <span class="check">✔</span>';
}
const prog = FQ.get()[STAGE_ID];
if(prog.math) markTabDone('math');
if(prog.latihan.done) markTabDone('latihan');
if(prog.boss.done) markTabDone('boss');

const warmupItems = [
  { q:'Sebuah segitiga siku-siku punya dua sisi tegak lurus 3 dan 4. Berapa panjang sisi miringnya?', type:'fill', answer:5, tol:0.1,
    hint:'Pythagoras: c = √(3²+4²) = √(9+16) = √25', explain:'c = √25 = 5.' },
  { q:'Dua gaya tegak lurus besarnya 6 N dan 8 N. Berapa resultannya?', type:'fill', answer:10, tol:0.2,
    hint:'R = √(6²+8²) = √(36+64) = √100', explain:'R = √100 = 10 N.' },
  { q:'Nilai sin 37° adalah...', options:['3/5','4/5','1/2','√2/2'], answer:0, hint:'Ingat segitiga 3-4-5: sin 37° = depan/miring = 3/5.', explain:'sin 37° = 3/5 = 0,6.' },
  { q:'Nilai cos 53° sama dengan...', options:['3/5','4/5','1','0'], answer:0, hint:'cos 53° = sin 37° (sudut saling berpenyiku) = 3/5.', explain:'cos 53° = 3/5.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderMathWarmup(document.getElementById('warmupBox'), warmupItems, {
  onDone(c,t){ FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'math',true); markTabDone('math'); FQ.toast(`Pemanasan selesai! ${c}/${t}`); }
});

const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.concept-card');
let cardIdx=0;
function showCard(i){
  cardIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(cards.length-1,i));
  cards.forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('active'));
  cards[cardIdx].classList.add('active');
  [document.getElementById('dots'),document.getElementById('dots2')].forEach(d=>{
    d.innerHTML = Array.from(cards).map((_,j)=>`<span class="${j===cardIdx?'on':''}"></span>`).join('');
  });
  document.getElementById('prevCard').disabled = cardIdx===0;
  if(cardIdx===cards.length-1) FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'materi',true);
}
document.getElementById('prevCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx-1);});
document.getElementById('nextCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx+1);});
showCard(0);

const practiceItems = [
  { q:'Arah garis gaya listrik selalu...', options:['Keluar dari (+) dan masuk ke (−)','Keluar dari (−) dan masuk ke (+)','Sejajar semua ke kanan','Acak, tergantung pengamat'], answer:0, explain:'Konvensi: garis gaya keluar dari muatan positif, masuk ke muatan negatif.' },
  { q:'Dua garis gaya listrik dapat saling berpotongan?', options:['Bisa, jika muatannya besar','Tidak pernah','Bisa, di titik tengah','Hanya pada medan homogen'], answer:1, explain:'Garis gaya tidak pernah berpotongan karena arah medan di satu titik hanya satu.' },
  { q:'Dua medan listrik tegak lurus besarnya 3 N/C dan 4 N/C. Medan totalnya adalah...', options:['5 N/C','7 N/C','1 N/C','12 N/C'], answer:0, explain:'√(3²+4²) = √25 = 5 N/C.' },
  { q:'Semakin rapat garis-garis gaya pada suatu daerah, artinya...', options:['Medan makin lemah','Medan makin kuat','Muatan netral','Tidak ada hubungan'], answer:1, explain:'Kerapatan garis gaya menggambarkan besar/kuat medan di daerah tersebut.' },
  { q:'sin 30° × 2 = ...', options:['1','2','0,5','√3'], answer:0, explain:'sin 30° = 1/2, maka 1/2 × 2 = 1.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderPractice(document.getElementById('practiceBox'), practiceItems, {
  onDone(c,t){ FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'latihan',{done:true,score:c}); markTabDone('latihan'); FQ.toast(`Latihan selesai! Skor ${c}/${t}`); }
});

const bossSteps = [
  { q:'Sebuah muatan uji diletakkan di dekat muatan positif besar. Ke arah mana garis gaya di titik itu?', weight:20,
    options:['Menjauhi muatan positif','Mendekati muatan positif','Sejajar permukaan bumi'], answer:0,
    explain:'Garis gaya selalu keluar (menjauh) dari muatan positif.' },
  { q:'Dua medan listrik E₁=6 N/C dan E₂=8 N/C saling tegak lurus di suatu titik. Berapa medan resultannya?', weight:30,
    options:['14 N/C','2 N/C','10 N/C','48 N/C'], answer:2,
    explain:'R = √(6²+8²) = √100 = 10 N/C (segitiga 6-8-10, kelipatan 3-4-5).' },
  { q:'Sebuah muatan q = 4×10⁻⁶ C menghasilkan medan di titik berjarak r = 0,2 m (k=9×10⁹). Berapa E?', weight:50,
    options:['9×10⁵ N/C','9×10⁴ N/C','4,5×10⁵ N/C','9×10³ N/C'], answer:0,
    explain:'E = 9×10⁹ × 4×10⁻⁶/(0,2)² = 9×10⁹×4×10⁻⁶/0,04 = 9×10⁵ N/C.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderBoss(document.getElementById('bossBox'), bossSteps, {
  bossName:'Roh Medan', bossEmoji:'🌀',
  onWin(){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'boss',{done:true,stars:3});
    markTabDone('boss'); FQ.sfx.unlock();
    setTimeout(()=>FQ.toast('Wilayah 3: Gua Gauss telah terbuka! 🕳️'), 800);
  }
});

        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">① Apa Fungsi Kapasitor?</h2>
        <p>Kapasitor berfungsi <b>menyimpan muatan listrik</b> dan <b>energi</b>. Bayangkan seperti tangki air yang diisi oleh pompa (baterai/sumber tegangan).</p>
        <p class="center"><span class="formula">C = q / V</span> &nbsp; &nbsp; <span class="formula">W = ½ C V²</span></p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 220 150" width="220">
            <rect x="70" y="30" width="80" height="100" fill="none" stroke="#3be8e0" stroke-width="3"/>
            <rect x="74" y="70" width="72" height="56" fill="rgba(59,232,224,.35)"/>
            <rect x="20" y="60" width="30" height="20" fill="#f6b93b"/>
            <text x="35" y="74" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" fill="#120c1f">pompa</text>
            <line x1="50" y1="70" x2="70" y2="70" stroke="#f6b93b" stroke-width="3"/>
            <text x="110" y="145" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#f3ead2">kapasitor = tangki muatan</text>
          </svg>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="concept-card" data-i="1">
        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">② Apa yang Memengaruhi Kapasitas?</h2>
        <p class="center"><span class="formula" style="font-size:1.1rem;">C = ε₀ <span class="frac"><span class="num">A</span><span class="den">d</span></span></span></p>
        <div class="visual-box">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 240 130" width="240">
            <rect x="40" y="20" width="14" height="90" fill="#ff6b6b"/>
            <rect x="140" y="20" width="14" height="90" fill="#5b8dff"/>
            <text x="95" y="20" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="#f3ead2">d</text>
            <line x1="54" y1="65" x2="140" y2="65" stroke="#f6b93b" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="3,3"/>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <ul>
          <li>Luas pelat (<span class="formula">A</span>) makin besar → <b>C makin besar</b></li>
          <li>Jarak antar pelat (<span class="formula">d</span>) makin kecil (didekatkan) → <b>C makin besar</b></li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="concept-card" data-i="2">
        <h2 style="margin-top:0;">③ Rangkaian Seri &amp; Paralel</h2>
        <div class="row">
          <div>
            <h3>Seri (muatan sama)</h3>
            <p class="center"><span class="formula"><span class="frac"><span class="num">1</span><span class="den">Cₛ</span></span> = <span class="frac"><span class="num">1</span><span class="den">C₁</span></span> + <span class="frac"><span class="num">1</span><span class="den">C₂</span></span> + ...</span></p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3>Paralel (tegangan sama)</h3>
            <p class="center"><span class="formula">C_p = C₁ + C₂ + ...</span></p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p class="small">💡 Trik cepat: kapasitor <b>seri</b> selalu menghasilkan kapasitas <b>total lebih kecil</b> dari kapasitor terkecilnya. Kapasitor <b>paralel</b> selalu menghasilkan kapasitas total <b>lebih besar</b> dari yang terbesar.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="card-nav">
        <button class="pixel-btn ghost" id="prevCard" style="font-size:.6rem;">← Sebelumnya</button>
        <div class="dots" id="dots2"></div>
        <button class="pixel-btn cyan" id="nextCard" style="font-size:.6rem;">Selanjutnya →</button>

  { q:'Berapa hasil dari 1/2 + 1/3 ? <span class="small">(KPK dari 2 dan 3 adalah 6)</span>', options:['2/5','5/6','1/6','3/5'], answer:1,
    hint:'1/2 = 3/6 dan 1/3 = 2/6, jadi 3/6+2/6 = 5/6.', explain:'1/2 + 1/3 = 3/6 + 2/6 = 5/6.' },
  { q:'Berapa hasil dari 1/4 + 1/4 ?', type:'fill', answer:0.5, tol:0.01, hint:'1/4 + 1/4 = 2/4 = 1/2 = 0,5', explain:'1/4+1/4 = 1/2 = 0,5.' },
  { q:'Dua kapasitor 4 μF dan 4 μF disusun seri. Berapa 1/Cs?', options:['1/2','2','1/8','8'], answer:0,
    hint:'1/Cs = 1/4 + 1/4 = 2/4 = 1/2', explain:'1/Cs = 1/4+1/4 = 1/2, maka Cs = 2 μF.' },
  { q:'Jika 1/Cs = 1/2, berapa Cs?', type:'fill', answer:2, tol:0.05, hint:'Cs adalah kebalikan dari 1/2.', explain:'Cs = 1/(1/2) = 2.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderMathWarmup(document.getElementById('warmupBox'), warmupItems, {
  onDone(c,t){ FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'math',true); markTabDone('math'); FQ.toast(`Pemanasan selesai! ${c}/${t}`); }
});

const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.concept-card');
let cardIdx=0;
function showCard(i){
  cardIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(cards.length-1,i));
  cards.forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('active'));
  cards[cardIdx].classList.add('active');
  [document.getElementById('dots'),document.getElementById('dots2')].forEach(d=>{
    d.innerHTML = Array.from(cards).map((_,j)=>`<span class="${j===cardIdx?'on':''}"></span>`).join('');
  });
  document.getElementById('prevCard').disabled = cardIdx===0;
  if(cardIdx===cards.length-1) FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'materi',true);
}
document.getElementById('prevCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx-1);});
document.getElementById('nextCard').addEventListener('click', ()=>{FQ.sfx.click(); showCard(cardIdx+1);});
showCard(0);

const practiceItems = [
  { q:'Fungsi utama kapasitor adalah...', options:['Menghambat arus saja','Menyimpan muatan dan energi listrik','Mengubah AC ke DC','Menghasilkan medan magnet'], answer:1, explain:'Kapasitor menyimpan muatan (q=CV) dan energi (W=½CV²).' },
  { q:'Jika jarak antar pelat kapasitor diperkecil, kapasitasnya akan...', options:['Mengecil','Membesar','Tetap','Menjadi nol'], answer:1, explain:'C=ε₀A/d, d mengecil → C membesar (berbanding terbalik).' },
  { q:'Dua kapasitor disusun paralel akan memiliki kapasitas total...', options:['Lebih kecil dari yang terkecil','Sama dengan rata-rata','Lebih besar dari yang terbesar','Selalu nol'], answer:2, explain:'Cp = C1+C2+..., selalu lebih besar dari komponen terbesarnya.' },
  { q:'Pada rangkaian seri kapasitor, besaran yang sama di tiap kapasitor adalah...', options:['Tegangan','Muatan','Kapasitas','Energi'], answer:1, explain:'Pada rangkaian seri, muatan (q) tiap kapasitor sama besar.' },
  { q:'Dua kapasitor 2 μF disusun paralel. Berapa kapasitas totalnya?', options:['1 μF','2 μF','4 μF','0,5 μF'], answer:2, explain:'Cp = 2+2 = 4 μF.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderPractice(document.getElementById('practiceBox'), practiceItems, {
  onDone(c,t){ FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'latihan',{done:true,score:c}); markTabDone('latihan'); FQ.toast(`Latihan selesai! Skor ${c}/${t}`); }
});

const bossSteps = [
  { q:'Apa yang terjadi pada kapasitas kapasitor keping sejajar jika luas pelat (A) diperbesar?', weight:20,
    options:['C mengecil','C membesar','C tetap','C menjadi negatif'], answer:1,
    explain:'C=ε₀A/d, A besar → C besar (berbanding lurus).' },
  { q:'Tiga kapasitor identik 6 μF disusun paralel. Berapa kapasitas totalnya?', weight:30,
    options:['2 μF','6 μF','18 μF','1/18 μF'], answer:2,
    explain:'Cp = 6+6+6 = 18 μF.' },
  { q:'Dua kapasitor 3 μF dan 6 μF disusun seri. Berapa kapasitas totalnya?', weight:50,
    options:['9 μF','4,5 μF','2 μF','1/9 μF'], answer:2,
    explain:'1/Cs = 1/3+1/6 = 2/6+1/6 = 3/6 = 1/2, maka Cs = 2 μF.' },
];
FQ_Quiz.renderBoss(document.getElementById('bossBox'), bossSteps, {
  bossName:'Penjaga Kastil', bossEmoji:'🛡️',
  onWin(){
    FQ.set(STAGE_ID,'boss',{done:true,stars:3});
    markTabDone('boss'); FQ.sfx.unlock();
    setTimeout(()=>FQ.toast('Wilayah 6: Menara Akhir telah terbuka! 🗼'), 800);
  }
});

        <p>Bayangkan potensial seperti <b>peta topografi</b>. Muatan <b>positif</b> membuat "bukit" potensial (V tinggi di dekatnya), muatan <b>negatif</b> membuat "lembah" potensial (V rendah/negatif di dekatnya).</p>

  document.querySelector('.wrap').innerHTML = `<div class="locked-note pixel-panel"><h2>🔒 Wilayah Terkunci</h2><p>Selesaikan dulu Wilayah 3: Gua Gauss.</p><a href="index.html" class="pixel-btn cyan">Kembali ke Peta</a></div>`;
  throw new Error('locked');
}

  FQ.sfx.click();
  tabs.forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tabpane').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('pane-'+btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
}));
function markTabDone(tab){
  const b = document.querySelector(`#tabbar button[data-tab="${tab}"]`);
  if(b && !b.querySelector('.check')) b.innerHTML += ' <span class="check">✔</span>';
}
const prog = FQ.get()[STAGE_ID];
if(prog.math) markTabDone('math');
if(prog.latihan.done) markTabDone('latihan');
if(prog.boss.done) markTabDone('boss');

  { q:'Berapa hasil dari 4 + (−7) ?', type:'fill', answer:-3, tol:0.1, hint:'4 + (−7) sama dengan 4 − 7.', explain:'4 + (−7) = −3.' },
  { q:'Jika V₁ = −2 dan V₂ = 6, berapa (V₂ − V₁)?', type:'fill', answer:8, tol:0.1, hint:'V₂ − V₁ = 6 − (−2) = 6 + 2', explain:'6 − (−2) = 8.' },
  { q:'q = −2 C, (V₂−V₁) = 5 V. Berapa W = q(V₂−V₁)?', type:'fill', answer:-10, tol:0.5, hint:'W = (−2) × 5', explain:'W = (−2) × 5 = −10 J.' },
];

});

  { q:'Potensial listrik (V) termasuk besaran...', options:['Vektor','Skalar','Tidak keduanya','Tensor'], answer:1, explain:'V adalah besaran skalar, hanya punya nilai (bisa +/−), tanpa arah.' },
  { q:'Muatan negatif akan menghasilkan potensial di sekitarnya yang bernilai...', options:['Selalu positif','Negatif (seperti lembah)','Nol','Tak terhingga'], answer:1, explain:'Muatan negatif membuat V bernilai negatif — seperti "lembah" potensial.' },
  { q:'Rumus usaha memindahkan muatan adalah...', options:['W = qV²','W = q(V₂−V₁)','W = q/V','W = V/q'], answer:1, explain:'W = q(V₂−V₁), usaha untuk memindahkan muatan q dari titik 1 ke titik 2.' },
  { q:'Jika q=3 C dan (V₂−V₁)=−4 V, maka W = ...', options:['−12 J','12 J','−1 J','7 J'], answer:0, explain:'W = 3 × (−4) = −12 J.' },
  { q:'Semakin dekat ke muatan positif, nilai potensialnya cenderung...', options:['Semakin kecil','Semakin besar (semakin positif)','Selalu nol','Berubah jadi negatif'], answer:1, explain:'Mendekati muatan positif, V = kq/r membesar karena r mengecil.' },

    explain:'V dan Eₚ adalah besaran skalar.' },
  { q:'Sebuah muatan q=5 C berpindah dari titik dengan V₁=2 V ke V₂=10 V. Berapa usaha W?', weight:30,
    options:['12 J','40 J','8 J','60 J'], answer:1,
    explain:'W = q(V₂−V₁) = 5×(10−2) = 5×8 = 40 J.' },

    options:['1,8×10⁵ V','1,8×10⁴ V','5,4×10⁵ V','9×10⁴ V'], answer:0,

  { q:'Jarak diperkecil menjadi 1/5 kali, nilai y menjadi...', options:['5 kali','1/5 kali','25 kali','1/25 kali'], answer:2, explain:'1/(1/5)²=25.' },

      <div class="card">
        <h3><span class="badge">C</span>Capacitance, in one line</h3>
        <p class="body-text"><strong>Capacitance</strong> tells you how much charge a capacitor can hold for every volt pushed across it. A bigger capacitance means more charge stored at the same voltage.</p>
        <p class="body-text" style="margin-bottom:0"><code class="formula">C = <span class="frac"><span class="num">Q</span><span class="den">V</span></span></code> &nbsp; Unit: <strong>Farad (F)</strong>. One Farad is huge, so real capacitors are usually measured in µF (microfarad), nF (nanofarad) or pF (picofarad).</p>
      </div>

      <div class="card tint-electric">
        <h3><span class="badge" style="background:var(--field)">?</span>Quick check</h3>
        <p class="body-text" style="margin-bottom:0">If you keep the capacitance the same but <strong>double the voltage</strong>, what happens to the stored charge Q? Try it on the sliders above and see for yourself.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

      <h2 class="section-title">What's inside a capacitor?</h2>
      <p class="lede">Every capacitor is really just two conducting plates, separated by a thin insulating layer called the <strong>dielectric</strong>. Change the shape below to see what affects capacitance.</p>

        <h3><span class="badge">A</span>Parallel-plate capacitor</h3>
        <p class="body-text">For flat plates facing each other:</p>
        <p class="body-text"><code class="formula">C = <span class="frac"><span class="num">ε<sub>0</sub> · ε<sub>r</sub> · A</span><span class="den">d</span></span></code></p>

          </div>

      <div class="card tint-field">
        <h3><span class="badge" style="background:var(--field)">i</span>What just happened?</h3>
        <p class="body-text" style="margin-bottom:0">Bigger plates → more room to store charge → capacitance goes <strong>up</strong>. Plates further apart → weaker attraction between them → capacitance goes <strong>down</strong>. A better dielectric lets the plates "feel" each other more strongly, so capacitance goes <strong>up</strong> too.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

          <div class="opt">Have no effect<span class="dot"></span></div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="true">Increase its capacitance<span class="dot"></span></div>
          <div class="opt">Make capacitance zero<span class="dot"></span></div>
          <p class="explain">More plate area gives more room to hold charge, so capacitance increases.</p>
        </div>
