<!-- Teacher modal -->
<div class="modal-overlay" id="teacherModal">
  <div class="modal">
    <h3>Running this in class</h3>
    <p class="sub">A simple 4-step flow · about 35–40 minutes total</p>

    <div class="step">
      <div class="num">1</div>
      <div class="txt"><strong>Open together (5 min)</strong>
      <p>Project the <em>Home</em> tab. Let one student drag the Voltage and Capacitance sliders while the class watches the plates fill with charge.</p></div>
    </div>
    <div class="step">
      <div class="num">2</div>
      <div class="txt"><strong>Explore in pairs (10 min)</strong>
      <p>Students open <em>Inside</em> and <em>Circuit</em> on their own devices, changing area, distance, dielectric, R and C, and note what increases capacitance.</p></div>
    </div>
    <div class="step">
      <div class="num">3</div>
      <div class="txt"><strong>Discuss real uses (10 min)</strong>
      <p>Browse the <em>Types</em> tab as a class. Ask: which capacitor type would you choose for a camera flash, and why?</p></div>
    </div>
    <div class="step">
      <div class="num">4</div>
      <div class="txt"><strong>Quiz individually (10–15 min)</strong>
      <p>Students complete the <em>Quiz</em> tab alone, then compare scores with a neighbour before whole-class review.</p></div>
    </div>

    <button class="btn btn-primary modal-close" onclick="document.getElementById('teacherModal').classList.remove('show')">Got it, close</button>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
/* ---------- Navigation ---------- */
const navBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn');
const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel');
navBtns.forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    navBtns.forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    panels.forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target).classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('appBody').scrollTo({top:0, behavior:'smooth'});
  });
});

/* ---------- Formatting helpers ---------- */
function formatCharge(uC){ // input already in microcoulombs
  if(uC >= 1000) return (uC/1000).toFixed(2)+' <span>mC</span>';
  return uC.toFixed(1)+' <span>µC</span>';
}
function formatFarads(F){
  if(F >= 1e-3) return (F*1e3).toFixed(2)+' mF';
  if(F >= 1e-6) return (F*1e6).toFixed(2)+' µF';
  if(F >= 1e-9) return (F*1e9).toFixed(2)+' nF';
  return (F*1e12).toFixed(2)+' pF';
}
function formatEnergy(J){
  if(J >= 1e-3) return (J*1e3).toFixed(2)+'<span>mJ</span>';
  if(J >= 1e-6) return (J*1e6).toFixed(2)+'<span>µJ</span>';
  return (J*1e9).toFixed(2)+'<span>nJ</span>';
}

/* ---------- HOME: hero plates ---------- */
const vSlider = document.getElementById('vSlider');
const cSlider = document.getElementById('cSlider');
const vLabel = document.getElementById('vLabel');
const cLabel = document.getElementById('cLabel');
const qReadout = document.getElementById('qReadout');
const fieldA = document.getElementById('fieldA');
const fieldB = document.getElementById('fieldB');
const fieldLines = document.querySelectorAll('#fieldLines .line');

function renderHero(){
  const V = parseFloat(vSlider.value);
  const C = parseFloat(cSlider.value); // µF
  vLabel.textContent = V.toFixed(1)+' V';
  cLabel.textContent = C.toFixed(0)+' µF';
  const Q = C * V; // µF * V = µC
  qReadout.innerHTML = formatCharge(Q);

  const intensity = Math.min(1, Q / 6000); // normalize for visuals
  const dotsCount = Math.round(intensity*6)+1;
  fieldA.innerHTML=''; fieldB.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=0;i<dotsCount;i++){
    const a=document.createElement('span'); a.className='charge-mark'; a.textContent='+'; a.style.opacity=1;
    fieldA.appendChild(a);
    const b=document.createElement('span'); b.className='charge-mark'; b.textContent='–'; b.style.opacity=1;
    fieldB.appendChild(b);
  }
  fieldLines.forEach((line,i)=>{
    line.style.opacity = intensity>0.05 ? Math.min(1,intensity+0.15) : 0;
    line.style.transform = `scaleX(${0.3+intensity*0.7})`;
  });
}
vSlider.addEventListener('input', renderHero);
cSlider.addEventListener('input', renderHero);
renderHero();

/* ---------- INSIDE: parallel plate ---------- */
const areaSlider = document.getElementById('areaSlider');
const distSlider = document.getElementById('distSlider');
const dielectricSelect = document.getElementById('dielectricSelect');
const areaLabel = document.getElementById('areaLabel');
const distLabel = document.getElementById('distLabel');
const parallelResult = document.getElementById('parallelResult');
const plateLeft = document.getElementById('plateLeft');
const plateRight = document.getElementById('plateRight');
const dielectricRect = document.getElementById('dielectricRect');

const EPS0 = 8.854e-12;

function renderParallelPlate(){
  const A_cm2 = parseFloat(areaSlider.value);
  const d_mm = parseFloat(distSlider.value);
  const epsR = parseFloat(dielectricSelect.value);
  areaLabel.textContent = A_cm2+' cm²';
  distLabel.textContent = d_mm.toFixed(1)+' mm';

  const A_m2 = A_cm2 * 1e-4;
  const d_m = d_mm * 1e-3;
  const C = EPS0 * epsR * A_m2 / d_m; // Farads
  parallelResult.innerHTML = formatFarads(C);

  // update diagram: gap width maps to distance, plate height maps to area
  const gap = 20 + (d_mm/10)*50; // px
  const height = 40 + (A_cm2/100)*70;
  const cx = 94;
  plateLeft.setAttribute('x', cx-gap/2-14);
  plateLeft.setAttribute('y', 70-height/2);
  plateLeft.setAttribute('height', height);
  plateRight.setAttribute('x', cx+gap/2);
  plateRight.setAttribute('y', 70-height/2);
  plateRight.setAttribute('height', height);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('x', cx-gap/2);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('width', gap);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('y', 70-height/2);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('height', height);
}
[areaSlider,distSlider,dielectricSelect].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('input', renderParallelPlate));
renderParallelPlate();

/* ---------- CIRCUIT: charging canvas ---------- */
const canvas = document.getElementById('circuitCanvas');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
const rSlider = document.getElementById('rSlider');
const cCircuitSlider = document.getElementById('cCircuitSlider');
const rLabel = document.getElementById('rLabel');
const cCircuitLabel = document.getElementById('cCircuitLabel');
const tauReadout = document.getElementById('tauReadout');

let animState = 'idle'; // idle | charging | discharging
let animStart = 0;
const ANIM_DURATION = 2600; // ms, durasi visual animasi

function getTau(){
  const R_kohm = parseFloat(rSlider.value);
  const C_uF = parseFloat(cCircuitSlider.value);
  return (R_kohm*1000) * (C_uF*1e-6);
}

function renderCircuitLabels(){
  rLabel.textContent = parseFloat(rSlider.value).toFixed(1)+' kΩ';
  cCircuitLabel.textContent = parseFloat(cCircuitSlider.value).toFixed(0)+' µF';
  tauReadout.textContent = getTau().toFixed(2)+' s';
}

// Saat slider digeser, reset animasi agar grafik tidak error
[rSlider,cCircuitSlider].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('input', ()=>{
  renderCircuitLabels();
  animState = 'idle';
  drawCircuit(0); // Gambar ulang dalam keadaan kosong
}));
renderCircuitLabels();

function currentFrac(){
  if(animState === 'idle') return 0; 
  const t = (performance.now() - animStart) / ANIM_DURATION;
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t));
}

function drawCircuit(frac){
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);

  // --- 1. MENGGAMBAR DIAGRAM SIRKUIT ---
  // Koordinat batas kotak sirkuit
  const leftX = 60, rightX = 320, topY = 50, botY = 140;

  ctx.strokeStyle = '#3A4166';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

  // Kabel Kiri & Baterai
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(leftX, topY);
  ctx.lineTo(leftX, 85);
  ctx.moveTo(leftX, 105);
  ctx.lineTo(leftX, botY);
  ctx.stroke();

  ctx.strokeStyle = '#8C96B8';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(leftX - 12, 85); ctx.lineTo(leftX + 12, 85); // Terminal + (panjang)
  ctx.moveTo(leftX - 7, 105); ctx.lineTo(leftX + 7, 105); // Terminal - (pendek)
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#8C96B8'; ctx.font = '11px IBM Plex Mono';
  ctx.fillText('V', leftX - 24, 99);
  ctx.fillText('+', leftX + 16, 88);

  // Kabel Atas & Resistor
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#3A4166';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(leftX, topY);
  ctx.lineTo(150, topY);
  // Garis Zig-zag Resistor
  ctx.lineTo(158, topY - 8); ctx.lineTo(174, topY + 8);
  ctx.lineTo(190, topY - 8); ctx.lineTo(206, topY + 8);
  ctx.lineTo(222, topY - 8); ctx.lineTo(230, topY);
  ctx.lineTo(rightX, topY);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#FFB300';
  ctx.fillText('R', 185, topY - 14);

  // Kabel Bawah (Ground)
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#3A4166';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(leftX, botY);
  ctx.lineTo(rightX, botY);
  ctx.stroke();

  // Kabel Kanan & Kapasitor
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(rightX, topY);
  ctx.lineTo(rightX, 85); // Turun ke pelat atas
  ctx.moveTo(rightX, 105); // Dari pelat bawah turun
  ctx.lineTo(rightX, botY);
  ctx.stroke();

  // Menghitung voltase saat ini untuk mengatur intensitas warna pelat
  let voltageLevel = 0;
  if(animState === 'charging'){
     voltageLevel = 1 - Math.exp(-5 * frac);
  } else if(animState === 'discharging'){
     voltageLevel = Math.exp(-5 * frac);
  }

  const glow = Math.max(0.1, voltageLevel);
  
  // Pelat Atas (+)
  ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(74,58,255,${0.2 + glow*0.8})`;
  ctx.fillRect(rightX - 18, 81, 36, 6);
  
  // Pelat Bawah (-)
  ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(20,199,165,${0.2 + glow*0.8})`;
  ctx.fillRect(rightX - 18, 103, 36, 6);
  
  ctx.fillStyle = '#5B6478';
  ctx.fillText('C', rightX + 22, 97);

  // --- 2. MENGGAMBAR GRAFIK V(t) ---
  const gX = 60, gY = 220, gW = 260, gH = 50;
  
  // Sumbu X dan Y
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#26304F';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(gX, gY); ctx.lineTo(gX + gW, gY); // X
  ctx.moveTo(gX, gY); ctx.lineTo(gX, gY - gH - 10); // Y
  ctx.stroke();
  
  ctx.fillStyle = '#5B6478'; ctx.font = '10px IBM Plex Mono';
  ctx.fillText('V(t)', gX - 28, gY - gH);
  ctx.fillText('t', gX + gW + 6, gY + 4);

  // Kurva Grafik
  if(animState !== 'idle') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#4A3AFF';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    
    const steps = 100;
    // Batasi seberapa jauh garis digambar (membuat efek animasi berjalan dari kiri ke kanan)
    const drawLimit = Math.floor(frac * steps); 

    for(let i = 0; i <= drawLimit; i++){
      const t = i / steps; 
      let v = 0;
      
      // 5 tau (5 konstanta waktu) = kapasitor dianggap penuh ~99.3%
      if(animState === 'charging'){
        v = 1 - Math.exp(-5 * t); 
      } else if (animState === 'discharging'){
        v = Math.exp(-5 * t);
      }
      
      const px = gX + t * gW;
      const py = gY - (v * gH);
      
      if(i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
      else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}
drawCircuit(0); 

function animateFrame(){
  const frac = currentFrac();
  drawCircuit(frac);
  
  if(frac < 1){
    requestAnimationFrame(animateFrame);
  }
}

document.getElementById('chargeBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  animState = 'charging'; 
  animStart = performance.now();
  requestAnimationFrame(animateFrame);
});

document.getElementById('dischargeBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  animState = 'discharging'; 
  animStart = performance.now();
  requestAnimationFrame(animateFrame);
});

/* ---------- TYPES: combine capacitors ---------- */
let combineMode='parallel';
const c1Input=document.getElementById('c1Input');
const c2Input=document.getElementById('c2Input');
const combineResult=document.getElementById('combineResult');
const combineFormula=document.getElementById('combineFormula');

function setCombineMode(mode){
  combineMode=mode;
  document.getElementById('btnParallel').classList.toggle('active', mode==='parallel');
  document.getElementById('btnSeries').classList.toggle('active', mode==='series');
  
  // Memasukkan HTML pecahan dan subscript
  combineFormula.innerHTML = mode==='parallel'
    ? '<code class="formula">C<sub>total</sub> = C<sub>1</sub> + C<sub>2</sub></code>'
    : '<code class="formula"><span class="frac"><span class="num">1</span><span class="den">C<sub>total</sub></span></span> = <span class="frac"><span class="num">1</span><span class="den">C<sub>1</sub></span></span> + <span class="frac"><span class="num">1</span><span class="den">C<sub>2</sub></span></span></code>';
  
  renderCombine();
}
function renderCombine(){
  const c1=parseFloat(c1Input.value)||0;
  const c2=parseFloat(c2Input.value)||0;
  let total;
  if(combineMode==='parallel'){ total=c1+c2; }
  else { total = (c1>0&&c2>0) ? (c1*c2)/(c1+c2) : 0; }
  combineResult.innerHTML = total.toFixed(1)+'<span>µF</span>';
}
[c1Input,c2Input].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('input', renderCombine));
renderCombine();

/* ---------- TYPES: energy stored ---------- */
const ecSlider=document.getElementById('ecSlider');
const evSlider=document.getElementById('evSlider');
const ecLabel=document.getElementById('ecLabel');
const evLabel=document.getElementById('evLabel');
const energyResult=document.getElementById('energyResult');
function renderEnergy(){
  const C_uF=parseFloat(ecSlider.value);
  const V=parseFloat(evSlider.value);
  ecLabel.textContent=C_uF.toFixed(0)+' µF';
  evLabel.textContent=V.toFixed(1)+' V';
  const C_F = C_uF*1e-6;
  const E = 0.5*C_F*V*V;
  energyResult.innerHTML = formatEnergy(E);
}
[ecSlider,evSlider].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('input', renderEnergy));
renderEnergy();

/* ---------- QUIZ ---------- */
let score=0;
const answeredMC = new Set();
const answeredFill = new Set();
function updateScore(){
  document.getElementById('scoreVal').textContent = score;
}
document.querySelectorAll('.q-block[data-qid]').forEach(block=>{
  const qid=block.dataset.qid;
  const opts=block.querySelectorAll('.opt');
  opts.forEach(opt=>{
    opt.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      if(answeredMC.has(qid)) return;
      answeredMC.add(qid);
      const isCorrect = opt.dataset.correct==='true';
      opts.forEach(o=>{
        if(o.dataset.correct==='true') o.classList.add('correct');
        else if(o===opt) o.classList.add('wrong');
      });
      if(isCorrect) score++;
      updateScore();
      block.querySelector('.explain').classList.add('show');
    });
  });
});
function checkFill(btn, accepted){
  const block = btn.closest('.q-block');
  const fid = block.dataset.fid;
  const input = block.querySelector('input[type=text]');
  const feedback = block.querySelector('.fill-feedback');
  const val = input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const isCorrect = accepted.includes(val);
  if(!answeredFill.has(fid)){
    answeredFill.add(fid);
    if(isCorrect) score++;
    updateScore();
  }
  feedback.textContent = isCorrect ? '✓ Correct!' : ('Not quite — try: '+accepted[0]);
  feedback.className = 'fill-feedback show ' + (isCorrect?'ok':'bad');
  input.disabled = true;
  btn.disabled = true;
}
function resetQuiz(){
  score=0; answeredMC.clear(); answeredFill.clear();
  updateScore();
  document.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(o=>o.classList.remove('correct','wrong'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.explain').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('show'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.fill-feedback').forEach(f=>{f.classList.remove('show','ok','bad'); f.textContent='';});
  document.querySelectorAll('.fill-row input').forEach(i=>{i.disabled=false; i.value='';});
  document.querySelectorAll('.fill-row button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);
  document.querySelector('textarea').value='';
}

/* close modal on backdrop click */
document.getElementById('teacherModal').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.id==='teacherModal') e.currentTarget.classList.remove('show');
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 4 — WHAT IS A CAPACITOR ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Foundations" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">04</span>Foundations</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">What is a Capacitor?</h2>
        <div class="demo-grid">
          <div>
            <p>A <strong>capacitor</strong> is a component that stores electric charge — and with it, electric potential energy — in an electric field.</p>
            <p>In its simplest form, it's just <strong>two conducting plates</strong> placed close together, separated by an insulating material called the <strong>dielectric</strong>.</p>
            <p style="margin-bottom:0;">No charge ever crosses the gap. Instead, energy is stored in the field between the plates.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="demo-stage">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 260 160" width="100%" style="max-width:260px;">
              <rect x="90" y="20" width="14" height="120" rx="3" fill="#E85D4C"/>
              <rect x="156" y="20" width="14" height="120" rx="3" fill="#3E6E9E"/>
              <rect x="104" y="24" width="52" height="112" fill="#FFB300" opacity="0.18"/>
              <text x="97" y="150" fill="#E85D4C" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle">+Q</text>
              <text x="163" y="150" fill="#3E6E9E" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle">−Q</text>
              <text x="130" y="12" fill="#FFB300" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="10" text-anchor="middle">dielectric</text>
            </svg>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 5 — WHY IT WORKS ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Foundations" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">05</span>Foundations</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Why can it store charge?</h2>
        <p class="lead">When the plates are connected to a battery, electrons are pushed onto one plate and pulled off the other one — through the wires, never through the gap.</p>
        <div class="card tint-steel">
          <div style="display:flex; gap:24px; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap;">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 240 110" width="100%" style="max-width:280px;">
              <line x1="10" y1="55" x2="70" y2="55" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="2"/>
              <line x1="170" y1="55" x2="230" y2="55" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="2"/>
              <rect x="72" y="10" width="10" height="90" rx="2" fill="#E85D4C"/>
              <rect x="158" y="10" width="10" height="90" rx="2" fill="#3E6E9E"/>
              <path d="M30 55 L58 55" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="1.6" marker-end="url(#arrow)"/>
              <path d="M182 55 L210 55" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="1.6" marker-end="url(#arrow)"/>
              <defs><marker id="arrow" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="4" refY="4" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 Z" fill="#1E2A38"/></marker></defs>
              <text x="10" y="30" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="9" fill="#5C6B7A">e⁻ leave</text>
              <text x="176" y="30" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="9" fill="#5C6B7A">e⁻ arrive</text>
            </svg>
            <p style="margin:0; flex:1; min-width:220px;">One plate ends up with excess positive charge <strong>+Q</strong>, the other with equal and opposite charge <strong>−Q</strong>. The insulating dielectric stops the charges from jumping across and cancelling out — so the charge just sits there, stored, until the circuit is closed again.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 6 — QUICK CHECK T/F ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Foundations" data-time="2">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">06</span>Quick Check</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 2 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">True or False?</h2>
        <p class="lead" style="font-size:1.1rem; color:var(--ink);">"The dielectric between the plates is a good electrical conductor."</p>
        <div class="opt-grid" style="max-width:420px;" data-quiz="tf1">
          <div class="opt" data-correct="false"><span class="dotmark"></span>True</div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="true"><span class="dotmark"></span>False</div>
        </div>
        <div class="explain" data-explain-for="tf1">Correct — the dielectric is an <strong>insulator</strong>, not a conductor. That's exactly why charge can't cross the gap and instead builds up on the plates, storing energy in the field.</div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 7 — DEFINE CAPACITANCE ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Foundations" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">07</span>Foundations</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Defining Capacitance</h2>
        <p class="lead"><strong>Capacitance (C)</strong> tells you how much charge a capacitor can store for every volt applied across it.</p>
        <div class="card tint" style="display:flex; gap:24px; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div style="display:inline-block; background:#fff; border:2px solid var(--steel); border-radius:10px; text-align:center; font-family:'Space Grotesk'; font-weight:700; overflow:hidden; flex:none;">
            <div style="border-bottom:2px solid var(--steel); padding:10px 30px; background:#EAF2F8; color:var(--coral); font-size:1.3rem;">Q</div>
            <div style="display:flex;">
              <div style="padding:10px 20px; border-right:2px solid var(--steel); flex:1; color:var(--steel); font-size:1.3rem;">C</div>
              <div style="padding:10px 20px; flex:1; color:var(--steel); font-size:1.3rem;">V</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="flex:1; min-width:240px;">
            <p style="margin:0 0 8px;"><strong>Magic Triangle:</strong> cover the letter you want. Top over bottom = divide. Side-by-side = multiply.</p>
            <div class="mjx">\( C = \dfrac{Q}{V} \quad|\quad Q = C \times V \quad|\quad V = \dfrac{Q}{C} \)</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="formula-box">
          <div class="mjx">\( C = \dfrac{Q}{V} \)</div>
          <div class="meta"><strong>Q</strong> = charge stored (Coulombs, C)<br><strong>V</strong> = potential difference (Volts, V)<br><strong>C</strong> = capacitance (Farads, F)</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 8 — UNITS ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Foundations" data-time="2">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">08</span>Foundations</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 2 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Units of Capacitance</h2>
        <p class="lead">The Farad (F) is <strong>huge</strong> — one Farad would be an enormous capacitor. Real components are usually measured in much smaller units:</p>
        <div class="factor-grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);">
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">1 mF</span><p>10⁻³ F<br>millifarad</p></div>
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">1 µF</span><p>10⁻⁶ F<br>microfarad</p></div>
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">1 nF</span><p>10⁻⁹ F<br>nanofarad</p></div>
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">1 pF</span><p>10⁻¹² F<br>picofarad</p></div>
        </div>
        <p style="color:var(--ink-soft); font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:10px;">Named after Michael Faraday. Most capacitors you'll see in this course are in the µF (microfarad) range.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 9 — WORKED EXAMPLE 1 ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Foundations" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">09</span>Worked Example</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Try it: find the capacitance</h2>
        <div class="card">
          <p style="margin-top:0;">A capacitor stores a charge of \( 6 \times 10^{-4} \text{ C} \) when connected across a 12 V battery. Calculate its capacitance.</p>
          <p style="color:var(--ink-soft); font-size:0.88rem;">Work it out on paper first — then reveal the steps.</p>
          <button class="btn btn-amber reveal-btn">Show Working ▾</button>
          <div class="reveal-box">
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">1</span><span>Given: \( Q = 6 \times 10^{-4}\text{ C} \), \( V = 12\text{ V} \)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">2</span><span>Use \( C = \dfrac{Q}{V} \)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">3</span><span>\( C = \dfrac{6\times10^{-4}}{12} = 5\times10^{-5}\text{ F} = 50\ \mu\text{F} \)</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 10 — QUICK MCQ ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="define" data-label="Quick Check" data-time="2">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">10</span>Quick Check</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 2 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">A 470 µF capacitor is connected to a 9 V supply. How much charge does it store?</h2>
        <div class="opt-grid" data-quiz="mcq1">
          <div class="opt" data-correct="false"><span class="dotmark"></span>52.2 µC</div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="true"><span class="dotmark"></span>4230 µC (4.23 × 10⁻³ C)</div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="false"><span class="dotmark"></span>4230 F</div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="false"><span class="dotmark"></span>0.9 µC</div>
        </div>
        <div class="explain" data-explain-for="mcq1">\( Q = C \times V = 470\times10^{-6} \times 9 = 4.23\times10^{-3}\text{ C} = 4230\ \mu\text{C} \)</div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 11 — PARALLEL PLATE ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Geometry" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">11</span>Geometry</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">The Parallel-Plate Capacitor</h2>
        <p class="lead">For two flat plates facing each other, capacitance depends only on their <strong>geometry</strong> and the material between them.</p>
        <div class="formula-box">
          <div class="mjx">\( C = \dfrac{\varepsilon_0 \varepsilon_r A}{d} \)</div>
          <div class="meta">\(\varepsilon_0 = 8.85\times10^{-12}\text{ F/m}\) &nbsp;·&nbsp; \(\varepsilon_r\) = dielectric constant (1 for air) &nbsp;·&nbsp; \(A\) = plate area (m²) &nbsp;·&nbsp; \(d\) = plate separation (m)</div>
        </div>
        <div class="factor-grid">
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">Area (A) ↑</span><p>Bigger plates → more room for charge → C increases.</p></div>
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">Distance (d) ↓</span><p>Closer plates → stronger field → C increases.</p></div>
          <div class="factor"><span class="tag">Dielectric (εᵣ) ↑</span><p>A better insulator lets plates hold more charge → C increases.</p></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 12 — INTERACTIVE DEMO ================= -->

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 13 — PREDICT & DISCUSS ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Geometry" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">13</span>Turn &amp; Talk</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Predict &amp; Discuss</h2>
        <p class="lead" style="font-size:1.05rem; color:var(--ink);">"If you double the plate area <em>and</em> keep everything else the same, what happens to the capacitance?"</p>
        <div class="timer-widget">
          <div class="timer-display" id="timerDisplay13">01:00</div>
          <div class="timer-btns">
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm timer-start" data-target="13">▶ Start</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm timer-reset" data-target="13">↺ Reset</button>
          </div>
          <span style="color:var(--ink-soft); font-size:0.85rem;">Discuss with a partner while the timer runs.</span>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-amber reveal-btn" style="margin-top:14px;">Reveal Answer ▾</button>
        <div class="reveal-box"><p style="margin:0;">Doubling A means \( C = \frac{\varepsilon_0\varepsilon_r A}{d} \) also <strong>doubles</strong>, since C is directly proportional to A. Twice the plate area = twice the room to store charge.</p></div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 14 — SERIES ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Circuits" data-time="4">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">14</span>Circuits</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 4 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Capacitors in Series</h2>
        <div class="demo-grid">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 220 120" width="100%" style="max-width:260px;">
            <line x1="10" y1="60" x2="60" y2="60" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="65" y1="42" x2="65" y2="78" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="4"/>
            <line x1="75" y1="42" x2="75" y2="78" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="4"/>
            <text x="70" y="95" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle">C₁</text>
            <line x1="80" y1="60" x2="140" y2="60" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="145" y1="42" x2="145" y2="78" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="4"/>
            <line x1="155" y1="42" x2="155" y2="78" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="4"/>
            <text x="150" y="95" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle">C₂</text>
            <line x1="160" y1="60" x2="210" y2="60" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <text x="110" y="20" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5C6B7A">V (battery)</text>
          </svg>
          <div class="panel series">
            <div class="mjx-panel">\( \dfrac{1}{C_{\text{total}}} = \dfrac{1}{C_1} + \dfrac{1}{C_2} + \dots \)</div>
            <ul>
              <li>Every capacitor stores the <strong>same charge</strong>: \(Q_1 = Q_2 = Q_{\text{total}}\)</li>
              <li>The total voltage <strong>splits</strong> across each capacitor.</li>
              <li>Total capacitance is always <strong>smaller</strong> than the smallest individual C.</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 15 — PARALLEL ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Circuits" data-time="4">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">15</span>Circuits</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 4 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Capacitors in Parallel</h2>
        <div class="demo-grid">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 220 130" width="100%" style="max-width:260px;">
            <line x1="30" y1="20" x2="30" y2="110" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="170" y1="20" x2="170" y2="110" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="30" y1="40" x2="80" y2="40" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="85" y1="30" x2="85" y2="50" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="4"/>
            <line x1="95" y1="30" x2="95" y2="50" stroke="#3E6E9E" stroke-width="4"/>
            <text x="115" y="44" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11">C₁</text>
            <line x1="100" y1="40" x2="170" y2="40" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="30" y1="80" x2="80" y2="80" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <line x1="85" y1="70" x2="85" y2="90" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="4"/>
            <line x1="95" y1="70" x2="95" y2="90" stroke="#E85D4C" stroke-width="4"/>
            <text x="115" y="84" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11">C₂</text>
            <line x1="100" y1="80" x2="170" y2="80" stroke="#1E2A38" stroke-width="2"/>
            <text x="100" y="126" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5C6B7A">V (battery)</text>
          </svg>
          <div class="panel parallel">
            <div class="mjx-panel">\( C_{\text{total}} = C_1 + C_2 + \dots \)</div>
            <ul>
              <li>Every capacitor feels the <strong>same voltage</strong>: \(V_1 = V_2 = V_{\text{total}}\)</li>
              <li>The total charge <strong>splits</strong> across each capacitor.</li>
              <li>Total capacitance is always <strong>larger</strong> than any individual C.</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 16 — COMPARE ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Circuits" data-time="2">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">16</span>Compare</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 2 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Series vs. Parallel — side by side</h2>
        <div class="two-col">
          <div class="panel series"><h3>Series</h3><p style="margin:0; font-size:0.9rem;">Same charge on each capacitor. Voltage divides. Behaves like adding <em>resistance to charging</em> — total C shrinks.</p></div>
          <div class="panel parallel"><h3>Parallel</h3><p style="margin:0; font-size:0.9rem;">Same voltage on each capacitor. Charge divides. Behaves like <em>more plate area</em> — total C grows.</p></div>
        </div>
        <p style="color:var(--ink-soft); font-size:0.9rem; margin-top:10px;">Ask the class: "why do series combinations always give a smaller total, while parallel gives a larger one?"</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 17 — WORKED EX: SERIES ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Worked Example" data-time="4">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">17</span>Worked Example</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 4 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Series circuit: work it out</h2>
        <div class="card">
          <p style="margin-top:0;">\( C_1 = 6\ \mu\text{F} \), \( C_2 = 3\ \mu\text{F} \), supply \( V = 18\text{ V} \), connected in series. Find \(C_{\text{total}}\), \(Q_{\text{total}}\), and the voltage across each capacitor.</p>
          <button class="btn btn-amber reveal-btn">Show Working ▾</button>
          <div class="reveal-box">
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">1</span><span>\( C_{\text{total}} = \dfrac{C_1 C_2}{C_1+C_2} = \dfrac{6\times3}{6+3} = 2\ \mu\text{F} \)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">2</span><span>\( Q_{\text{total}} = C_{\text{total}} \times V = 2\times18 = 36\ \mu\text{C} \) (same on both, since series)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">3</span><span>\( V_1 = Q/C_1 = 36/6 = 6\text{ V} \) &nbsp;·&nbsp; \( V_2 = Q/C_2 = 36/3 = 12\text{ V} \) &nbsp;→&nbsp; \(6+12=18\text{ V}\) ✓</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 18 — WORKED EX: PARALLEL ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="geo" data-label="Worked Example" data-time="4">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">18</span>Worked Example</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 4 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Parallel circuit: work it out</h2>
        <div class="card">
          <p style="margin-top:0;">\( C_3 = 4\ \mu\text{F} \), \( C_4 = 2\ \mu\text{F} \), supply \( V = 10\text{ V} \), connected in parallel. Find \(C_{\text{total}}\) and the charge on each capacitor.</p>
          <button class="btn btn-amber reveal-btn">Show Working ▾</button>
          <div class="reveal-box">
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">1</span><span>\( C_{\text{total}} = C_3+C_4 = 4+2 = 6\ \mu\text{F} \)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">2</span><span>Both feel the same 10 V (parallel).</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">3</span><span>\( Q_3 = C_3 V = 4\times10 = 40\ \mu\text{C} \) &nbsp;·&nbsp; \( Q_4 = C_4 V = 2\times10 = 20\ \mu\text{C} \)</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 19 — BRIDGE POLL ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="energy" data-label="Bridge" data-time="2">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">19</span>Quick Poll</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 2 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Between those two circuits, which one stores more total energy?</h2>
        <div class="opt-grid" style="max-width:460px;" data-quiz="poll19">
          <div class="opt" data-correct="false"><span class="dotmark"></span>The series circuit (C₁, C₂)</div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="true"><span class="dotmark"></span>The parallel circuit (C₃, C₄)</div>
          <div class="opt" data-correct="false"><span class="dotmark"></span>They store exactly the same</div>
        </div>
        <div class="explain" data-explain-for="poll19">Good question to hold onto! We'll calculate both in a moment — but here's a hint: the parallel circuit has a much larger equivalent capacitance (6 µF vs 2 µF). Let's find out if that means more stored energy.</div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 20 — ENERGY FORMULA ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="energy" data-label="Energy" data-time="4">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">20</span>Energy</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 4 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Energy Stored in a Capacitor</h2>
        <p class="lead">Charging a capacitor takes work — each new bit of charge is pushed against the charge already on the plates. That work becomes stored energy.</p>
        <div class="demo-grid">
          <div class="formula-box" style="flex-direction:column; align-items:flex-start;">
            <div class="mjx">\( U = \dfrac{1}{2}QV = \dfrac{1}{2}CV^2 = \dfrac{Q^2}{2C} \)</div>
            <div class="meta">Pick whichever form uses the variables you already know. U is in Joules (J).</div>
          </div>
          <div class="demo-stage">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 200 160" width="100%" style="max-width:200px;">
              <line x1="30" y1="20" x2="30" y2="140" stroke="#8C96B8" stroke-width="1.5"/>
              <line x1="30" y1="140" x2="180" y2="140" stroke="#8C96B8" stroke-width="1.5"/>
              <polygon points="30,140 160,140 30,30" fill="#FFB300" opacity="0.35"/>
              <line x1="30" y1="140" x2="160" y2="30" stroke="#FFB300" stroke-width="2.5"/>
              <text x="10" y="30" fill="#8C96B8" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="9">Q</text>
              <text x="170" y="152" fill="#8C96B8" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="9">V</text>
              <text x="70" y="115" fill="#FFB300" font-family="IBM Plex Mono" font-size="10">U = area</text>
            </svg>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p style="color:var(--ink-soft); font-size:0.87rem;">Think of it like a Q–V graph: the energy stored is the <strong>area of the triangle</strong> under the line, which is why there's a ½ in the formula.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 21 — WORKED EX: ENERGY ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="energy" data-label="Worked Example" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">21</span>Worked Example</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Try it: find the energy stored</h2>
        <div class="card">
          <p style="margin-top:0;">A 220 µF capacitor is charged to 25 V. Calculate the energy stored.</p>
          <button class="btn btn-amber reveal-btn">Show Working ▾</button>
          <div class="reveal-box">
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">1</span><span>\( C = 220\times10^{-6}\text{ F} \), \( V=25\text{ V} \) → use \( U=\frac12 CV^2 \)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">2</span><span>\( U = 0.5 \times 220\times10^{-6} \times 25^2 \)</span></div>
            <div class="step-line"><span class="dot">3</span><span>\( U \approx 0.0688\text{ J} = 68.8\text{ mJ} \)</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

          <div class="timer-btns">
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm timer-start" data-target="23">▶ Start</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm timer-reset" data-target="23">↺ Reset</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-amber reveal-btn" style="margin-top:14px;">Reveal a Model Answer ▾</button>
        <div class="reveal-box"><p style="margin:0;">A small battery can't push out a burst of current fast enough to make a bright flash instantly. By slowly charging a capacitor first, all that stored energy can be released almost instantly — much faster than the battery could deliver it directly.</p></div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 24 — RECAP ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="reflect" data-label="Recap" data-time="3">
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="tag-row"><span class="num-badge"><span class="sq">24</span>Recap</span><span class="time-chip">⏱ 3 min</span></div>
        <h2 class="title">Formula Recap</h2>
        <div class="card dark">
          <div class="recap-grid">
            <div class="recap-item"><div class="mjx-recap">\( C=\frac{Q}{V} \)</div><p>Definition</p></div>
            <div class="recap-item"><div class="mjx-recap">\( C=\frac{\varepsilon_0\varepsilon_r A}{d} \)</div><p>Parallel-plate</p></div>
            <div class="recap-item"><div class="mjx-recap">\( \frac{1}{C_t}=\Sigma\frac{1}{C_i} \)</div><p>Series</p></div>
            <div class="recap-item"><div class="mjx-recap">\( C_t=\Sigma C_i \)</div><p>Parallel</p></div>
            <div class="recap-item"><div class="mjx-recap">\( U=\frac12 CV^2 \)</div><p>Stored energy</p></div>
            <div class="recap-item"><div class="mjx-recap">\( U=\frac{Q^2}{2C} \)</div><p>Stored energy (alt)</p></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ================= SLIDE 25 — EXIT TICKET ================= -->
    <section class="slide" data-cat="reflect" data-label="Exit Ticket" data-time="3">

<script>
/* ============ CATEGORY COLORS ============ */
const CAT_COLORS = {
  warmup:  {color:'#E85D4C', label:'Warm-Up'},
  define:  {color:'#E89A00', label:'Foundations'},
  geo:     {color:'#3E6E9E', label:'Geometry & Circuits'},
  energy:  {color:'#7A4FB0', label:'Energy'},
  apply:   {color:'#4CAF8C', label:'Real World'},
  reflect: {color:'#1E2A38', label:'Recap & Wrap-Up'}
};

/* ============ SLIDE NAVIGATION ============ */
const slides = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.slide'));
let current = 0;
const total = slides.length;
const counterEl = document.getElementById('counter');
const meterFill = document.getElementById('meterFill');
const meterPct = document.getElementById('meterPct');
const catPill = document.getElementById('catPill');
const catLabel = document.getElementById('catLabel');
const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prevBtn');
const nextBtn = document.getElementById('nextBtn');
const stage = document.getElementById('stage');

function pad(n){ return n.toString().padStart(2,'0'); }

function render(){
  slides.forEach((s,i)=> s.classList.toggle('active', i===current));
  counterEl.textContent = pad(current+1)+' / '+pad(total);
  const pct = Math.round(((current+1)/total)*100);
  meterFill.style.width = pct+'%';
  meterPct.textContent = pct+'%';
  const cat = slides[current].dataset.cat;
  const info = CAT_COLORS[cat] || CAT_COLORS.warmup;
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--cat-color', info.color);
  catLabel.textContent = slides[current].dataset.label || info.label;
  prevBtn.disabled = current===0;
  nextBtn.disabled = current===total-1;
  stage.scrollTop = 0;
  const activeSlide = slides[current];
  if(activeSlide) activeSlide.scrollTop = 0;
  if(window.MathJax && window.MathJax.typesetPromise){ window.MathJax.typesetPromise([activeSlide]); }
}

function goTo(i){
  if(i<0 || i>=total) return;
  current = i;
  render();
}
prevBtn.addEventListener('click', ()=> goTo(current-1));
nextBtn.addEventListener('click', ()=> goTo(current+1));

document.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{
  if(e.key==='ArrowRight' || e.key===' '){ e.preventDefault(); goTo(current+1); }
  else if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){ e.preventDefault(); goTo(current-1); }
});

/* ============ ELAPSED CLOCK ============ */
const clockPill = document.getElementById('clockPill');
const startTime = performance.now();
setInterval(()=>{
  const elapsed = Math.floor((performance.now()-startTime)/1000);
  const mm = pad(Math.floor(elapsed/60));
  const ss = pad(elapsed%60);
  clockPill.textContent = '⏱ '+mm+':'+ss+' elapsed';
}, 1000);

/* ============ FULLSCREEN ============ */
document.getElementById('fsBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  if(!document.fullscreenElement){ document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().catch(()=>{}); }
  else { document.exitFullscreen(); }
});

/* ============ REVEAL BUTTONS (worked examples, predictions) ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.reveal-btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    const box = btn.nextElementSibling;
    const showing = box.classList.toggle('show');
    btn.textContent = showing ? 'Hide Working ▴' : (btn.dataset.label || 'Show Working ▾');
    if(showing && window.MathJax && window.MathJax.typesetPromise){ window.MathJax.typesetPromise([box]); }
  });
});
document.querySelectorAll('.reveal-btn').forEach(btn=>{
  if(btn.textContent.includes('Reveal')) btn.dataset.label = btn.textContent;
});

/* ============ QUIZ OPTIONS (with correct/wrong) ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('[data-quiz]').forEach(group=>{
  const qid = group.dataset.quiz;
  const explain = document.querySelector('.explain[data-explain-for="'+qid+'"]');
  let answered = false;
  group.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(opt=>{
    opt.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      if(answered) return;
      answered = true;
      const isCorrect = opt.dataset.correct === 'true';
      group.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(o=>{
        if(o.dataset.correct==='true') o.classList.add('correct');
        else if(o===opt) o.classList.add('wrong');
      });
      if(explain) explain.classList.add('show');
    });
  });
});

/* ============ POLL CARDS (no right/wrong, just highlight + reveal) ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('[data-poll]').forEach(card=>{
  card.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    const group = card.dataset.poll;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-poll="'+group+'"]').forEach(c=> c.classList.remove('picked'));
    card.classList.add('picked');
    if(group==='hook'){ document.getElementById('explainHook').classList.add('show'); }
    if(group==='conf'){ document.getElementById('explainConf').classList.add('show'); }
  });
});

/* ============ APP CARDS (flip / reveal) ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.app-card').forEach(card=>{
  card.addEventListener('click', ()=> card.classList.toggle('open'));
});

/* ============ TIMER WIDGETS (turn & talk) ============ */
const timerState = {};
function setupTimer(id, seconds){
  timerState[id] = {remaining: seconds, total: seconds, interval: null};
  updateTimerDisplay(id);
}
function updateTimerDisplay(id){
  const el = document.getElementById('timerDisplay'+id);
  if(!el) return;
  const s = timerState[id].remaining;
  const mm = pad(Math.floor(s/60));
  const ss = pad(s%60);
  el.textContent = mm+':'+ss;
}
function startTimer(id){
  const state = timerState[id];
  if(state.interval) return;
  state.interval = setInterval(()=>{
    state.remaining -= 1;
    if(state.remaining <= 0){
      state.remaining = 0;
      clearInterval(state.interval);
      state.interval = null;
    }
    updateTimerDisplay(id);
  }, 1000);
}
function resetTimer(id){
  const state = timerState[id];
  clearInterval(state.interval);
  state.interval = null;
  state.remaining = state.total;
  updateTimerDisplay(id);
}
setupTimer('13', 60);
setupTimer('23', 120);
document.querySelectorAll('.timer-start').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=> startTimer(btn.dataset.target));
});
document.querySelectorAll('.timer-reset').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=> resetTimer(btn.dataset.target));
});

/* ============ INTERACTIVE DEMO: PARALLEL PLATE ============ */
const EPS0 = 8.854e-12;
const areaSlider = document.getElementById('areaSlider');
const distSlider = document.getElementById('distSlider');
const dielectricSelect = document.getElementById('dielectricSelect');
const areaLabel = document.getElementById('areaLabel');
const distLabel = document.getElementById('distLabel');
const parallelResult = document.getElementById('parallelResult');
const plateLeft = document.getElementById('plateLeft');
const plateRight = document.getElementById('plateRight');
const dielectricRect = document.getElementById('dielectricRect');

function formatFarads(F){
  if(F >= 1e-3) return (F*1e3).toFixed(2)+' mF';
  if(F >= 1e-6) return (F*1e6).toFixed(2)+' µF';
  if(F >= 1e-9) return (F*1e9).toFixed(2)+' nF';
  return (F*1e12).toFixed(2)+' pF';
}
function renderParallelPlate(){
  if(!areaSlider) return;
  const A_cm2 = parseFloat(areaSlider.value);
  const d_mm = parseFloat(distSlider.value);
  const epsR = parseFloat(dielectricSelect.value);
  areaLabel.textContent = A_cm2+' cm²';
  distLabel.textContent = d_mm.toFixed(1)+' mm';
  const A_m2 = A_cm2 * 1e-4;
  const d_m = d_mm * 1e-3;
  const C = EPS0 * epsR * A_m2 / d_m;
  parallelResult.textContent = formatFarads(C);
  const gap = 20 + (d_mm/10)*50;
  const height = 40 + (A_cm2/100)*70;
  const cx = 94;
  plateLeft.setAttribute('x', cx-gap/2-14);
  plateLeft.setAttribute('y', 70-height/2);
  plateLeft.setAttribute('height', height);
  plateRight.setAttribute('x', cx+gap/2);
  plateRight.setAttribute('y', 70-height/2);
  plateRight.setAttribute('height', height);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('x', cx-gap/2);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('width', gap);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('y', 70-height/2);
  dielectricRect.setAttribute('height', height);
}
if(areaSlider){
  [areaSlider,distSlider,dielectricSelect].forEach(el=> el.addEventListener('input', renderParallelPlate));
  renderParallelPlate();
}

/* ============ INIT ============ */
render();
</script>

</body>
</html>
